=== game-api: module-data.ts [api-reference/game-api/module-data]
Declarations from module-data.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/module-data.ts.

## ModuleDataScope
Storage granularity for an authoritative module data schema: singleton `'world'`, per-`'player'`, or per-`'entity'`.
    export type ModuleDataScope = 'world' | 'player' | 'entity';
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleDataSchema
Namespaced identifier, integer schema version, and scope of a replicated module data store.
    export interface ModuleDataSchema {
      /** Stable module namespace plus local name, e.g. example.inventory/items. */
      readonly id: string;
      readonly version: number;
      readonly scope: ModuleDataScope;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## ModuleDataTarget
Address of a single module data record combining the namespaced `adapter` ID and record key `id`.
    export interface ModuleDataTarget { readonly adapter: string; readonly id: string }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## ModuleDataCommand
Authoritative mutation request targeting a module data record with a named `command` and JSON-compatible `args`.
    export interface ModuleDataCommand extends ModuleDataTarget { readonly command: string; readonly args: ModuleValue }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## ModuleDataActor
Authenticated caller context (`playerId`, optional target `ownerId`, and committed simulation `tick`) passed to `ModuleDataAdapter.authorize`.
    export interface ModuleDataActor { readonly playerId: string; readonly ownerId?: string; readonly tick: number }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleDataAdapter
Deterministic, synchronous, bounded callbacks. Values are detached and frozen. No IO, clocks or hidden mutable state. A reducer returns its entire next record atomically. All records are replicated to every peer: never put secrets/tokens in this store.
    export interface ModuleDataAdapter {
      readonly name: string;
      readonly version: number;
      readonly scope: ModuleDataScope;
      readonly initial: ModuleValue;
      validate(value: ModuleValue): boolean;
      /** One migration per schema version, from N to N+1. Used only when loading saves. */
      readonly migrations?: Readonly<Record<number, (value: ModuleValue) => ModuleValue>>;
      authorize(input: Readonly<ModuleDataActor & { target: ModuleDataTarget; command: string; args: ModuleValue; state: ModuleValue }>): boolean;
      reduce(input: Readonly<{ target: ModuleDataTarget; command: string; args: ModuleValue; state: ModuleValue; tick: number }>): ModuleValue;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleDataExtension
Resolved module data adapter contribution pairing a `ModuleDataSchema` with its deterministic `ModuleDataAdapter`.
    export interface ModuleDataExtension extends ModuleDataSchema { readonly adapter: ModuleDataAdapter }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## ModuleDataRecord
Detached, frozen record entry (`adapter`, `id`, and validated `value`) inside a module data snapshot or delta.
    export interface ModuleDataRecord extends ModuleDataTarget { readonly value: ModuleValue }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleDataSnapshot
Portable `space-sim-module-data/1` snapshot of all registered schemas and non-default records at a monotonic `revision`.
    export interface ModuleDataSnapshot {
      readonly format: 'space-sim-module-data/1';
      readonly revision: number;
      readonly schemas: readonly ModuleDataSchema[];
      readonly records: readonly ModuleDataRecord[];
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleDataDelta
Incremental or full `space-sim-module-data/1` replication payload; `since` names the base revision when sending deltas.
    export interface ModuleDataDelta extends ModuleDataSnapshot {
      /** Absent means complete replacement; present means apply only these changed records. */
      readonly since?: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleDataRead
Read-only view of authoritative module data records, schemas, snapshots, and revision deltas.
    export interface ModuleDataRead {
      readonly schemas: readonly ModuleDataSchema[];
      readonly revision: number;
      get(adapter: string, id: string): ModuleValue | undefined;
      snapshot(): ModuleDataSnapshot;
      delta(since: number): ModuleDataDelta;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleDataControl
Mutable authoritative module data store supporting direct host writes, authorized commands, network snapshot application, and migrated save restores.
    export interface ModuleDataControl extends ModuleDataRead {
      /** Trusted host initialization/update; remote callers use commands, never arbitrary writes. */
      set(target: ModuleDataTarget, value: ModuleValue): void;
      remove(target: ModuleDataTarget): void;
      command(request: ModuleDataCommand, actor: ModuleDataActor): void;
      /** Validate every record before committing. Network replication requires exact versions. */
      apply(snapshot: unknown): void;
      /** Save loads may use registered one-version migrations. Missing adapters are errors. */
      restore(snapshot: unknown): void;
      clear(): void;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleDataFactory
Headless storage for a session adapter's own world. The session supplies authenticated actor/ownership context; this factory grants no access to another live world.
    export interface ModuleDataFactory { create(adapters: readonly ModuleDataExtension[]): ModuleDataControl }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## CORE_MODULE_DATA
Service key for the authoritative module data store factory (`core.module-data@1.0.0`).
    export const CORE_MODULE_DATA: ServiceKey<ModuleDataFactory> = Object.freeze({slot:'core.module-data',version:'1.0.0'});
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

=== game-api: modules.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules]

## ModuleEnvironment

    export type ModuleEnvironment = 'browser' | 'offline' | 'node';

## Disposable
Resource cleanup handle with an idempotent `dispose()` method. Register module-owned handles with `context.own(...)`.
    export interface Disposable { dispose(): void }
Used by: builtin.audio, builtin.navball, spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.alarm-panel, spacelab.audio-controls, spacelab.camera-follow, spacelab.chat-console, spacelab.controller-menu, spacelab.discovery-lan, spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.hud-layout, spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-keyboard, spacelab.join-review, spacelab.local-pilots, spacelab.local-views, spacelab.logging, spacelab.maneuver-burn, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.manual, spacelab.mod-menu, spacelab.net-node-ws, spacelab.net-tab, spacelab.net-webrtc, spacelab.net-websocket, spacelab.orbital-alarms, spacelab.planet-designer, spacelab.player-controller, spacelab.player-view, spacelab.profiler, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.session-flight, spacelab.session-join, spacelab.spin-view, spacelab.theme, spacelab.theme-customizer, spacelab.tutorial-runner, spacelab.ui-shell, spacelab.ui-windows, spacelab.universe-files, spacelab.universe-save-controls, spacelab.universe-saves, spacelab.universe-server-storage, spacelab.view-hud.

## ModuleReference
An exact module version, as profiles and locks record it.
    export interface ModuleReference { readonly id: string; readonly version: string }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleDependency
A dependency or conflict: an exact version or a range such as `^1.2.0`, `~1.2.0`, `>=1.2.0 <2.0.0`, `*`, or alternatives joined by `||`. Prereleases match only ranges that name a prerelease of the same version. Resolution records the exact enabled version.
    export interface ModuleDependency { readonly id: string; readonly version: string }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## NumberSetting
Bounded numeric configuration parameter schema declared in a module manifest (`[minimum, maximum]`).
    export interface NumberSetting {
      readonly type: 'number';
      readonly label?: string;
      /** Plain-text help for module configuration controls. */
      readonly description?: string;
      /** Optional numeric increment, measured from minimum. Omitted means continuous. */
      readonly step?: number;
      readonly default: number;
      readonly minimum: number;
      readonly maximum: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.mod-menu.

## ModuleManifest
Legacy v1 module manifest (`space-sim-module/1`) targeting `space-sim-game-api/1`.
    export interface ModuleManifest {
      readonly format: 'space-sim-module/1';
      readonly apiVersion: 'space-sim-game-api/1';
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly version: string;
      readonly kind: 'provider' | 'data';
      readonly authority: 'authoritative' | 'presentation';
      readonly environments: readonly ModuleEnvironment[];
      /** Enabled before this module, which may use their exposed APIs. */
      readonly dependencies: readonly ModuleDependency[];
      /** Modules that cannot be enabled together with this one. */
      readonly conflicts?: readonly ModuleDependency[];
      readonly provides: readonly string[];
      readonly requires: readonly string[];
      readonly slots: readonly string[];
      readonly before: readonly string[];
      readonly after: readonly string[];
      readonly settings: Readonly<Record<string, NumberSetting>>;
      readonly license: string;
    }

## ModuleProfile
Legacy v1 profile (`space-sim-profile/1`) selecting enabled modules, slot providers, and numeric setting overrides.
    export interface ModuleProfile {
      readonly format: 'space-sim-profile/1';
      readonly id: string;
      readonly modules: readonly ModuleReference[];
      readonly providers: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
      readonly settings: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<Record<string, number>>>>;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleInitialization
Initialization is synchronous in Phase 2. Own resources immediately after allocation. provide() only accepts slots selected for this module by the resolved profile.
    export interface ModuleInitialization {
      /** Resolved values for the manifest's declared settings, after profile overrides and
       * range validation. Read them during initialization; a change prepares a new candidate. */
      readonly settings: Readonly<Record<string, number>>;
      /** Text assets embedded in an imported code artifact; empty for static modules. */
      readonly assets: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
      /** Own a sequential state migration in this candidate, without global registration. */
      migrateState(fromVersion: number, migrate: (value: ModuleValue) => ModuleValue): void;
      /** Register a resource for disposal with this candidate, in reverse acquisition order.
       * Disposal runs when the candidate is replaced or removed, including after a failed start. */
      own<T extends Disposable>(resource: T): T;
      /** True when the resolved profile selected this module as the provider of `slot`. */
      selected(slot: string): boolean;
      provide(slot: string, service: unknown): void;
      /** Authoritative static modules only; uses the resolved module identity and order. */
      contributeForce(hook: ForceHook): void;
      contributeWorldRule(hook: WorldRuleHook): void;
      /** Authoritative modules only: up to 32 named commands, run by the authoritative host. */
      contributeCommand(hook: CommandHook): void;
      /** Presentation modules only: up to eight of each, in resolved module order. */
      contributeLayer(hook: LayerHook): void;
      contributePanel(hook: PanelHook): void;
      contributeCamera(hook: CameraHook): void;
      /** Publish a read-only API (an object of functions and constants) for dependent modules.
       * At most once. Authoritative state still belongs in module state, never in closures. */
      expose(api: object): void;
      /** The API exposed by one of this module's declared dependencies, which initialize first. */
      dependency<T extends object>(id: string): T;
    }
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

## RegisteredModule

=== game-api: modules-v2.ts [api-reference/game-api/modules-v2]
Declarations from modules-v2.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/modules-v2.ts.

## ModuleTier
Where a module runs and what it may touch. `authoritative` code is deterministic simulation shared by saves and multiplayer; `presentation` code draws or plays per viewer; `platform` code owns devices, windows and files; `network` code owns transports and sessions. Authoritative modules may require only authoritative providers; presentation modules may require presentation or authoritative providers; platform and network modules may require any.
    export type ModuleTier = 'authoritative' | 'presentation' | 'platform' | 'network';
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## CapabilityRef
A provided service: its slot name and exact semver version, such as `hud.throttle` `1.0.0`.
    export interface CapabilityRef { readonly name: string; readonly version: string }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## CapabilityNeed
A required service. `version` is a semver range such as `^1.0.0`. An optional need may resolve without a provider; read it with `tryUse`. A required need fails resolution instead.
    export interface CapabilityNeed extends CapabilityRef { readonly optional?: boolean }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ManifestV2
New metadata has a separate parser and lock. Legacy manifests/locks keep their identity.
    export interface ManifestV2 {
      readonly format: 'space-sim-module/2';
      readonly apiVersion: 'space-sim-game-api/2';
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly version: string;
      readonly kind: 'provider' | 'data';
      readonly tier: ModuleTier;
      readonly environments: readonly ModuleEnvironment[];
      readonly dependencies: readonly ModuleReference[];
      readonly conflicts: readonly ModuleReference[];
      readonly provides: readonly CapabilityRef[];
      readonly requires: readonly CapabilityNeed[];
      /** Service slots this module can fill. A profile selects one provider per slot. */
      readonly slots: readonly string[];
      /** Module IDs this module must initialize before or after; both must be enabled. */
      readonly before: readonly string[];
      readonly after: readonly string[];
      readonly beforeIfPresent?: readonly string[];
      readonly afterIfPresent?: readonly string[];
      /** Numeric settings shown in module configuration; values reach `context.settings`. */
      readonly settings: Readonly<Record<string, NumberSetting>>;
      readonly license: string;
    }
Used by: spacelab.mod-menu.

## ModuleProfileV2
Dependencies use semver ranges despite sharing the reference record shape.
    export interface ModuleProfileV2 {
      readonly format: 'space-sim-profile/2';
      readonly id: string;
      readonly modules: readonly ModuleReference[];
      readonly providers: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
      /** Requester ID -> capability name -> explicitly chosen provider ID. */
      readonly capabilities: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<Record<string, string>>>>;
      readonly settings: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<Record<string, number>>>>;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## CapabilityBinding
Resolved capability binding pairing a required service slot and exact version with the selected provider module reference.
    export interface CapabilityBinding extends CapabilityRef { readonly provider: ModuleReference }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleLockV2
Resolved v2 module lock (`space-sim-lock/2`) recording enabled modules, topological initialization order, slot providers, capability bindings, and numeric settings.
    export interface ModuleLockV2 {
      readonly format: 'space-sim-lock/2';
      readonly profileId: string;
      readonly modules: readonly ModuleReference[];
      readonly order: readonly string[];
      readonly providers: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
      readonly capabilities: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<Record<string, CapabilityBinding>>>>;
      readonly settings: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<Record<string, number>>>>;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ServiceKey
Typed handle for one service slot and exact version, exported by game-api as a constant (for example `THROTTLE_HUD`). Pass it to `provide`, `use` or `tryUse`; never construct a look-alike key for a service you have not declared in the manifest. A phantom function makes service types invariant under strictFunctionTypes.
    export interface ServiceKey<T> {
      readonly slot: string;
      readonly version: string;
      readonly serviceType?: (value: T) => T;
    }
Used by: builtin.audio, builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.motion, builtin.navball, spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.alarm-panel, spacelab.audio-controls, spacelab.camera-follow, spacelab.camera-smart, spacelab.chat-console, spacelab.controller-menu, spacelab.discovery-lan, spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.hud-layout, spacelab.input-actions, spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-keyboard, spacelab.input-profiles, spacelab.input-touch, spacelab.local-pilots, spacelab.local-views, spacelab.logging, spacelab.maneuver-burn, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.manual, spacelab.mod-menu, spacelab.net-node-ws, spacelab.net-tab, spacelab.net-webrtc, spacelab.net-websocket, spacelab.orbital-alarms, spacelab.orbital-lessons, spacelab.planet-designer, spacelab.player-controller, spacelab.player-view, spacelab.profiler, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.session-flight, spacelab.session-join, spacelab.spin-view, spacelab.theme, spacelab.theme-customizer, spacelab.trainer, spacelab.trainer-appearance, spacelab.transfer-planner, spacelab.tutorial-runner, spacelab.ui-shell, spacelab.ui-windows, spacelab.universe-files, spacelab.universe-save-controls, spacelab.universe-saves, spacelab.universe-server-storage, spacelab.vector-shaders, spacelab.view-hud.

## ModuleAssets
Read-only package asset accessor for embedded UTF-8 text, raw bytes, or revocable blob URLs.
    export interface ModuleAssets {
      text(path: string): string;
      bytes(path: string): Uint8Array;

    }

## ModuleInitializationV2
Synchronous v2 module initialization context (`space-sim-game-api/2`) providing typed service lookup (`use`/`tryUse`), publication (`provide`), resource ownership (`own`), and host contributions.
    export interface ModuleInitializationV2 extends Omit<ModuleInitialization, 'assets' | 'provide'> {
      /** Present on current hosts; older v2 hosts may omit this additive diagnostic identity. */
      readonly module?: ModuleReference;
      readonly assets: ModuleAssets;
      /** Initialization only. Publishes this module's implementation of a declared `provides`
       * entry; throws unless the resolved profile selected this module for the slot. */
      provide<T>(key: ServiceKey<T>, service: T): void;
      /** Initialization only. Returns the bound provider for a declared `requires` entry, which
       * initialized first. Throws for an undeclared, unbound or version-incompatible key.
       * Keep the returned service; it remains valid until this candidate is disposed. */
      use<T>(key: ServiceKey<T>): T;
      /** Initialization only. Absence is allowed only for a declared optional capability
       * with no binding; invalid access and a bound provider's failures still throw. */
      tryUse<T>(key: ServiceKey<T>): T | undefined;
      contributeInput(source: InputSource): void;
      /** Contributes namespaced action definitions to the active candidate (platform tier only). */
      contributeActions(actions: readonly ActionDefinitionV2[]): void;
      contributeTransport(provider: TransportProvider): void;
      contributeDiscovery(provider: DiscoveryProvider): void;
      /** Authoritative records shared by saves and compatible session replication. */
      contributeData(adapter: ModuleDataAdapter): void;
      contributeVehicle(vehicle: VehicleDefinition): void;
      contributeVehicleAppearance(appearance: VehicleAppearance): void;
      contributeWindow(window: WindowDefinition): void;
      /** Bounded structured Manual data, available in every tier without a UI dependency. */
      contributeGuide(page: GuidePage): void;
      contributeMaterial(material: MaterialDefinition): void;
      contributePostEffect(effect: PostEffectDefinition): void;
    }
Used by: builtin.audio, builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.motion, builtin.navball, spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.alarm-panel, spacelab.audio-controls, spacelab.camera-follow, spacelab.camera-smart, spacelab.chat-console, spacelab.controller-menu, spacelab.discovery-lan, spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.hud-layout, spacelab.input-actions, spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-keyboard, spacelab.input-profiles, spacelab.input-touch, spacelab.join-review, spacelab.local-pilots, spacelab.local-views, spacelab.logging, spacelab.maneuver-burn, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.manual, spacelab.mod-menu, spacelab.net-node-ws, spacelab.net-tab, spacelab.net-webrtc, spacelab.net-websocket, spacelab.orbital-alarms, spacelab.orbital-lessons, spacelab.planet-designer, spacelab.player-controller, spacelab.player-view, spacelab.profiler, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.session-flight, spacelab.session-join, spacelab.spin-view, spacelab.theme, spacelab.theme-customizer, spacelab.throttle-hud, spacelab.trainer, spacelab.trainer-appearance, spacelab.transfer-planner, spacelab.tutorial-runner, spacelab.ui-shell, spacelab.ui-windows, spacelab.universe-files, spacelab.universe-save-controls, spacelab.universe-saves, spacelab.universe-server-storage, spacelab.vector-shaders, spacelab.view-hud.

=== game-api: motion.ts [api-reference/game-api/motion]
Declarations from motion.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/motion.ts.

## ModuleValue
JSON-serializable value primitive or tree stored in module state.
    export type ModuleValue = null | boolean | number | string | readonly ModuleValue[] | { readonly [key: string]: ModuleValue };
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ModuleState
Per-ship authoritative state. Each namespace is limited to 16 KiB of JSON.
    export interface ModuleState { readonly version: number; readonly value: ModuleValue }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## MotionStep
State and control input for advancing one ship by a fixed simulation time step in SI units.
    export interface MotionStep {
      readonly state: State;
      readonly controls: Controls;
      readonly seconds: number;
      readonly body: Body;
      readonly surface?: SurfaceProfile;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ForceEvaluation
Staged kinematic state and controls evaluated at integration kick stages in body-local coordinates.
    export interface ForceEvaluation {
      /** Scheduler boundary tick; time is the actual integrator stage epoch in seconds. */
      readonly tick: number;
      readonly time: number;
      readonly position: Vec;
      readonly velocity: Vec;
      readonly body: Body;
      readonly controls: Controls;
      readonly state: ModuleState;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ForceHook
Synchronous, pure callbacks: no hidden mutable authority, wall clock or random IO. Acceleration is body-local m/s², evaluated at KDK kicks (also fractional contacts). Each component is finite and at most 10,000 m/s² in magnitude. All evaluations in one outer step read the same state; commit runs once after verified motion. Inputs are detached/read-only. Exceptions or invalid outputs abort this ship's step. Work must be bounded by the author; synchronous JS cannot be preempted.
    export interface ForceHook {
      /** Include every force parameter in this local cache identity. */
      readonly modelIdentity: string;
      readonly initialState: ModuleState;
      evaluate(input: ForceEvaluation): Vec;
      commit?(input: Readonly<MotionStep & { readonly next: State; readonly moduleState: ModuleState }>): ModuleState;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ForceExtension
Identity and order come from the resolved registry, one hook per module.
    export interface ForceExtension {
      readonly moduleId: string;
      readonly version: string;
      readonly hook: ForceHook;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## NumericalKernel
The default numerical/contact kernel, with the active force schedule. A replacement may call it for verified terrain/water behavior, or supply its own bounded motion. Calling it does not commit hook state; the outer motion boundary owns that commit.
    export type NumericalKernel = (input: MotionStep) => State;
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## DynamicsServices
Services provided to replacement integrators, including the base numerical kernel and acceleration query.
    export interface DynamicsServices {
      readonly numerical: NumericalKernel;
      /** For replacement integrators that accept force contributions. Same staged state
       * and validation as the built-in kernel; position/velocity are stage values. */
      acceleration(time: number, position: Vec, velocity: Vec): Vec;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## DynamicsProvider
Integrator extension providing custom numerical or analytic flight stepping.
    export interface DynamicsProvider {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly version: string;
      readonly modelIdentity: string;
      readonly supportsForces: boolean;
      step(input: MotionStep, services: DynamicsServices): State;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## MotionContextOptions
Configuration options supplied when creating a per-ship or headless simulation motion context.
    export interface MotionContextOptions {
      readonly services?: Partial<MotionServices>;
      /** Shared-world hooks require ordinary stepping even for stationary ships. */
      readonly worldRules?: boolean;
      readonly worldRuleIdentity?: string;
      readonly dynamics?: DynamicsProvider;
      /** Already resolved dependency/before/after order, captured at construction. */
      readonly forces?: readonly ForceExtension[];
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## MotionCapabilities
Capabilities reported by a simulation context describing available analytic coasting, rails, and diagnostics.
    export interface MotionCapabilities {
      readonly analyticCoast: boolean;
      readonly rails: boolean;
      readonly orbitalDiagnostics: boolean;
      readonly legacyCompatible: boolean;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

=== game-api: node-transport.ts [api-reference/game-api/node-transport]
Declarations from node-transport.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/node-transport.ts.

## UpgradeByteStream
Borrowed native byte stream interface (e.g. Node.js `net.Socket`). Protocol providers do not own the HTTP server or port listener.
    export interface UpgradeByteStream {
      /** Number of bytes buffered for writing. */
      readonly writableLength: number;
      /** Whether the stream is open for writes. */
      readonly writable: boolean;
      /** Whether the underlying socket has been destroyed. */
      readonly destroyed: boolean;
      /** Enable or disable TCP_NODELAY (Nagle's algorithm). */
      setNoDelay?(enabled: boolean): unknown;
      /** Subscribe to data chunks received on the socket. */
      on(event: 'data', listener: (data: Uint8Array) => void): unknown;
      /** Subscribe to socket communication errors. */
      on(event: 'error', listener: (error: Error) => void): unknown;
      /** Subscribe to socket closure or termination events. */
      on(event: 'close' | 'end', listener: () => void): unknown;
      /** Write string or byte data to the socket. */
      write(data: string | Uint8Array): unknown;
      /** End the stream, optionally sending final data. */
      end(data?: string | Uint8Array): unknown;
      /** Destroy the stream immediately. */
      destroy(): unknown;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## HttpUpgrade
HTTP upgrade request headers passed from the Node HTTP server.
    export interface HttpUpgrade {
      /** HTTP method (usually 'GET'). */
      readonly method?: string;
      /** Case-insensitive HTTP request headers. */
      readonly headers: Readonly<Record<string, string | readonly string[] | undefined>>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## WebSocketCodecs
Node-only engine primitives; no HTTP/WebSocket policy or connection state.
    export interface WebSocketCodecs {
      /** Compute standard SHA-1 base64 hash for the Sec-WebSocket-Accept handshake. */
      sha1Base64(text: string): string;
      /** Raw DEFLATE with level 1 and sync-flush termination, including its final empty block. */
      deflate(bytes: Uint8Array): Uint8Array;
      /** Raw sync-flushed DEFLATE. Must reject output exceeding maximumBytes. */
      inflate(bytes: Uint8Array, maximumBytes: number): Uint8Array;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## NODE_WEBSOCKET_CODECS
ServiceKey for native WebSocket cryptographic and compression codecs (slot `core.websocket-codecs`).
    export const NODE_WEBSOCKET_CODECS: ServiceKey<WebSocketCodecs> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'core.websocket-codecs', version: '1.0.0' });
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## ServerSocketOptions
Configuration options for an accepted server WebSocket connection.
    export interface ServerSocketOptions {
      /** Maximum accepted message size in bytes before closing the connection. */
      readonly maxMessageBytes: number;
      /** Heartbeat ping interval in milliseconds. Zero or negative disables pings. */
      readonly pingIntervalMs: number;
      /** Diagnostic label identifying this server socket or endpoint. */
      readonly label: string;
      /** Whether permessage-deflate compression was negotiated. */
      readonly deflate?: boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## ServerTextConnection
Server-side text connection extending TextConnection with socket close notification.
    export interface ServerTextConnection extends TextConnection {
      /** Resolves when TCP has closed, including the bounded close-handshake grace period. */
      readonly closed: Promise<void>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## NodeWebSocketProvider
Server WebSocket provider capable of upgrading HTTP requests into ServerTextConnection instances.
    export interface NodeWebSocketProvider extends Disposable {
      /**
       * Accept one borrowed HTTP upgrade or refuse it with an HTTP response.
       * Returns undefined if the handshake headers or key are invalid.
       */
      accept(request: HttpUpgrade, stream: UpgradeByteStream, head: Uint8Array, options: ServerSocketOptions): ServerTextConnection | undefined;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

## NODE_WEBSOCKET_TRANSPORT
ServiceKey for the Node WebSocket server transport (slot `net.node-ws`).
    export const NODE_WEBSOCKET_TRANSPORT: ServiceKey<NodeWebSocketProvider> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'net.node-ws', version: '1.0.0' });
Used by: spacelab.net-node-ws.

=== game-api: platform.ts [api-reference/game-api/platform]
Declarations from platform.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/platform.ts.

## InputFrame
Device-local input frame delivered to input sources. `elapsed` is wall-clock seconds (never a simulation step dt). `pilot` is the local seat index (0–3).
    export interface InputFrame {
      /** Wall-clock seconds since the prior frame. Units: seconds. */
      readonly elapsed: number;
      /** Local seat index in [0, 3]. */
      readonly pilot: number;
      /** True when continuous flight control inputs should be sampled. */
      readonly flightAllowed: boolean;
      /** True when global UI and view shortcut actions should be sampled. */
      readonly shortcutsAllowed: boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## ActionSink
Synchronous receiver for action values written by an InputSource during sample().
    export interface ActionSink {
      /** Writes an analog or digital action value. Clamped by consumers to [-1, 1]. */
      set(action: string, value: number): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## ActionDefinitionV2
Namespaced action definition contributed by platform-tier input modules.
    export interface ActionDefinitionV2 {
      /** Namespaced action identifier (e.g. `spacelab.input-actions/engine`). */
      readonly id: string;
      /** Friendly human-readable label shown in control configuration menus. */
      readonly label: string;
      /** Action category grouping for UI filtering and suppression. */
      readonly category: 'flight' | 'warp' | 'view' | 'system';
      /** Action trigger mode: continuous hold, bipolar axis, or discrete press. */
      readonly mode: 'held' | 'axis' | 'press';
      /** Default KeyboardEvent.code bindings or modifier chords. */
      readonly defaultKeys: readonly string[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.input-actions, spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-keyboard, spacelab.input-touch.

## InputSource
Device sampler that translates hardware events or DOM interactions into namespaced actions.
    export interface InputSource extends Disposable {
      /** Human-readable name identifying the hardware source type (e.g. 'keyboard', 'gamepad', 'touch'). */
      readonly name: string;
      /** Synchronous sampling pass writing at most 256 action entries into the sink. */
      sample(frame: InputFrame, into: ActionSink): void;
      /** Releases held inputs for the specified local pilot seat, or all seats if omitted. */
      release(pilot?: number): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.input-gamepad, spacelab.input-keyboard, spacelab.input-touch.

## PilotRegistry
Registry managing active local player seats and input device assignments.
    export interface PilotRegistry {
      /** Number of active local player seats in [1, 4]. */
      readonly count: number;
      /** Returns the session player ID for the specified local seat index. */
      playerId(pilot: number): string | undefined;
      /** Assigns an input device ('auto', 'keyboard', 'none', or 'pad:N') to a seat. */
      assign(pilot: number, device: string): void;
      /** Releases held inputs for a seat or all seats. */
      release(pilot?: number): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.local-pilots.

## CORE_PILOTS

## LocalPilotHost
Host callbacks that read active session identities and device assignments. Assigning a device must never create a network player or grant session ownership.
    export interface LocalPilotHost {
      /** Returns active local player seat count in [1, 4]. */
      count(): number;
      /** Returns session player ID for a local seat index, if assigned. */
      playerId(pilot: number): string | undefined;
      /** Assigns an input device ('auto', 'keyboard', 'none', or 'pad:N') to a seat index. */

## ControlTargetBinding
Local routing identity connecting a seat and viewport to a session player and target entity.
    export interface ControlTargetBinding {
      /** Local seat identifier. */
      readonly seatId: string;
      /** Session player identifier. */
      readonly playerId: string;
      /** Target vessel or world entity identifier. */
      readonly entityId: string;
      /** Optional viewport identifier for multi-view split screen. */
      readonly viewportId?: string;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## ControlTargetAdapter
Explicit entity or session adapter for routing intent. Implementations validate ownership and execute actions without advancing simulation state. Schema must be 'spacelab-control-target/1'.
    export interface ControlTargetAdapter {
      /** Explicit adapter contract schema identifier. */
      readonly schema: 'spacelab-control-target/1';
      /** Returns true if the session player currently owns and can command the target entity. */
      canControl(binding: ControlTargetBinding): boolean;
      /** Returns the bounded list of namespaced action IDs accepted by this target (max 256). */
      actions(binding: ControlTargetBinding): readonly string[];
      /** Sends an updated action value in [-1, 1] to the controlled entity. */
      setAction(binding: ControlTargetBinding, action: string, value: number): void;
      /** Clears active intent associated with the specified binding. */
      release(binding: ControlTargetBinding): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## PlayerInputBinding
Input source binding for a single local player seat.
    export interface PlayerInputBinding {
      /** Local device seat index in [0, 3]. */
      readonly pilot: number;
      /** Borrowed, ordered input sources (at most 16). Last write wins; provider retains ownership. */
      readonly sources: readonly InputSource[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## PlayerController
Routes namespaced intent from input sources to a compatible control target adapter.
    export interface PlayerController extends Disposable {
      /** Active target binding, or undefined if no target is selected. */
      readonly target: ControlTargetBinding | undefined;
      /** Selects a target entity. Releasing or changing target clears prior intent. */
      select(target: ControlTargetBinding | undefined): void;
      /** Binds input sources for this seat. Reassignment clears held device state. */
      bindInput(input: PlayerInputBinding | undefined): void;
      /** Synchronously samples bound sources and dispatches changed actions to the adapter. */
      sample(frame: Omit<InputFrame, 'pilot'>): void;
      /** Manually sets an action value in [-1, 1] on the active target. */
      set(action: string, value: number): void;
      /** Releases all held actions and informs the adapter. */
      release(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

    export interface PlayerControllerFactory {
      /** Creates a player controller bound to the specified adapter and optional initial input. */
      create(adapter: ControlTargetAdapter, input?: PlayerInputBinding): PlayerController;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-controller.

## TextConnection
Bidirectional text transport connection with backpressure and lifecycle events. Shared by browser WebSockets, WebRTC data channels, scoped tab channels, and Node server sockets.
    export interface TextConnection {
      /** Human-readable peer label or endpoint address for diagnostics. */
      readonly label: string;
      /** Number of bytes currently queued but not yet transmitted over the wire. */
      readonly bufferedAmount: number;
      /** Transmit an application text message. Dropped or throws if the connection is closed. */
      send(text: string): void;
      /** Close the connection cleanly, optionally passing a reason message to the remote peer. */
      close(reason?: string): void;
      /** Handler invoked when a text message arrives from the remote peer. */
      onmessage: ((text: string) => void) | null;
      /** Handler invoked once when the connection closes, receiving the close reason. */
      onclose: ((reason: string) => void) | null;
    }
Used by: spacelab.discovery-lan, spacelab.net-node-ws, spacelab.net-tab, spacelab.net-webrtc, spacelab.net-websocket, spacelab.session-flight, spacelab.session-join.

## TransportProvider
Pluggable network transport provider capable of opening or listening for reliable text connections.
    export interface TransportProvider {
      /** Transport identifier, such as 'websocket' or 'tab'. */
      readonly name: string;
      /** Supported URL schemes, e.g. ['ws', 'wss'] or ['tab']. */
      readonly schemes: readonly string[];
      /** Signal owns pending IO and the established connection's lifetime. */
      connect(address: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<TextConnection>;
      /** Signal owns pending IO and the listener; accepted peers remain separately owned. */
      listen?(address: string, accept: (peer: TextConnection) => void, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Disposable>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.discovery-lan, spacelab.net-tab, spacelab.net-websocket.

## WEBSOCKET_TRANSPORT

    export const WEBSOCKET_TRANSPORT: ServiceKey<TransportProvider> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'net.websocket', version: '1.0.0' });

      readonly maxPlayers: number;

    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

=== game-api: player-view.ts [api-reference/game-api/player-view]
Declarations from player-view.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/player-view.ts.

## PlayerViewRole
Role identifier for a player view component adapter.
    export type PlayerViewRole = 'input' | 'camera' | 'hud' | 'appearance';
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## PlayerViewAdapter
One player's adapter instance. Configuration/schema belongs to its provider, allowing future editors without a shared bag of game-specific settings. The view owns this adapter; underlying borrowed services retain their owner.
    export interface PlayerViewAdapter extends Disposable {
      /** Stable underlying resource identity; prevents mutable instances crossing players. */
      readonly resource: object;
      rebind(binding: HudBinding): void;
      release(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-view.

## PlayerViewAdapters
Map of player view roles to their active adapter instances.
    export type PlayerViewAdapters = Readonly<Partial<Record<PlayerViewRole, PlayerViewAdapter>>>;
Used by: spacelab.player-view.

## PlayerView
Per-seat view composition coordinating input, camera, HUD, and appearance adapters with atomic rebind and release.
    export interface PlayerView extends Disposable {
      readonly binding: HudBinding;
      readonly adapters: PlayerViewAdapters;
      /** Release all old intent before rebinding. A failed rebind removes every adapter,
       * leaving a neutral view that can be configured again. Seat/view identity is fixed. */
      bind(binding: HudBinding): void;
      /** Replace only this player's parts. Failed preparation retains the old parts,
       * with their intent released; no game world is created or advanced. */
      configure(adapters: PlayerViewAdapters): void;
      release(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-view.

## PlayerAdapterOwnership
Resource lifecycle ownership policy for wrapped player view components.
    export type PlayerAdapterOwnership = 'borrowed' | 'owned';
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## PlayerViewFactory
Factory creating per-seat `PlayerView` instances and role adapters (`ui.player-view@1.0.0`).
    export interface PlayerViewFactory extends Disposable {
      create(options: { readonly binding: HudBinding; readonly adapters: PlayerViewAdapters }): PlayerView;
      controller(controller: PlayerController, ownership?: PlayerAdapterOwnership,
        target?: (binding: HudBinding) => ControlTargetBinding | undefined): PlayerViewAdapter;
      camera(camera: LocalViewCamera, ownership?: PlayerAdapterOwnership): PlayerViewAdapter;
      hud(hud: ViewHud, ownership?: PlayerAdapterOwnership): PlayerViewAdapter;
    }
Used by: spacelab.player-view.

## PLAYER_VIEWS
Service key for the player view composition factory (`ui.player-view@1.0.0`).
    export const PLAYER_VIEWS: ServiceKey<PlayerViewFactory> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'ui.player-view', version: '1.0.0' });
Used by: spacelab.player-view.

=== game-api: presentation.ts [api-reference/game-api/presentation]
Declarations from presentation.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/presentation.ts.

## Camera
System world coordinates (metres, +Y up) to CSS pixels: `scale` pixels per metre, `rotation` radians counter-clockwise applied to the world before projection.
    export interface Camera { readonly center: Vec; readonly rotation: number; readonly scale: number }
Used by: spacelab.camera-follow, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.theme.

## RenderBody
Celestial body descriptor for a rendered frame in system world coordinates (`position` and `radius` in `m`, `rotation` in `rad`), plus optional terrain and shader appearance.
    export interface RenderBody {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly position: Vec;
      readonly radius: number;
      /** Shared authoritative terrain geometry. */
      readonly surface?: SurfaceProfile;
      readonly color?: string;
      readonly accentColor?: string;
      readonly atmosphereColor?: string;
      readonly horizonColor?: string;
      readonly skyColor?: string;
      readonly surfaceFeatures?: 'globe-lines' | 'craters' | 'smooth' | 'star';
      readonly parentPosition?: Vec;
      readonly orbitRadius?: number;
      /** Drawn angle of the body-fixed frame (terrain, markings) in radians; absent or 0 when it does not spin. */
      readonly rotation?: number;
      /** Unit vector from the body toward its star, in system coordinates. Absent: drawn unlit. */
      readonly sunDirection?: Vec;
      /** Procedural surface look; absent draws a plain disc (low graphics quality). */
      readonly appearance?: BodyAppearance & { readonly colors: readonly string[] };
    }
Used by: spacelab.planet-designer, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## RenderShip
Another vessel in the same world: one of this player's fleet, or someone else's.
    export interface RenderShip {
      readonly vehicleId?: string;
      readonly id: string;
      /** System world coordinates. */
      readonly position: Vec;
      readonly heading: number;
      readonly status: State['status'];
      readonly engine: boolean;
      readonly throttle: number;
      readonly label: string;
      /** Owned by another player. */
      readonly foreign?: boolean;
      /** Drawn as a small unlabeled rock instead of a vessel (asteroids and debris). */
      readonly rock?: boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## RenderFrame
Complete scene description passed to `Renderer.draw` once per animation frame: interpolated `state`, `path` samples, `camera`, `bodies`, `ships`, `overlays`, and `theme`.
    export interface RenderFrame {
      readonly vehicleId?: string;
      /** Presentation snapshot; may be interpolated between fixed physics steps. */
      readonly state: State;
      /** Ghost samples with exact velocities. Closed loops are drawn whole; open paths start at the ship. */
      readonly path: readonly GhostSample[];
      readonly pathClosed: boolean;
      readonly camera: Camera;
      readonly engine: boolean;
      /** Celestial bodies in the active system (planets and moons). */
      readonly bodies?: readonly RenderBody[];
      /** Ship position in system world coordinates (defaults to state.position). */
      readonly shipWorldPosition?: Vec;
      /** Reference origin of ghost path (e.g. active body position). */
      readonly pathOrigin?: Vec;
      /** World position of launch pad if any. */
      readonly padPosition?: Vec;
      /** Outward normal at the pad, for its tangent marker. */
      readonly padNormal?: Vec;
      /** Label beside the piloted ship (default VESSEL 01). */
      readonly shipLabel?: string;
      /** Viewer-only annotations; omitted preserves the default visible labels and scale bar. */
      readonly showScale?: boolean;
      readonly showShip?: boolean;
      readonly showPad?: boolean;
      /** Optional explicit viewer animation epoch, seconds. Never advances a simulation. */
      readonly presentationTime?: number;
      readonly showShipLabels?: boolean;
      /** Celestial body names; defaults to visible. Independent of ship labels. */
      readonly showBodyLabels?: boolean;
      /** Planet/moon orbit guides; defaults to visible. Does not hide ship trajectories or affect motion. */
      readonly showBodyOrbits?: boolean;
      /** Other vessels, drawn beneath the piloted ship. */
      readonly ships?: readonly RenderShip[];
      /** Validated presentation layers above the scene, in the overlay section: shapes in order,
       * then text in order so labels stay readable. */
      readonly overlays?: readonly OverlayPrimitive[];
      /** Visual theme settings for universe backdrop, skybox, grids, and timers/counters. */
      readonly theme?: RenderThemeOptions;
    }
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## ThemeBackdropStyle
Named deep-space backdrop preset for scene rendering.
    export type ThemeBackdropStyle = 'spacelab' | 'nebula' | 'carina' | 'galactic' | 'aurora' | 'supernova' | 'void' | 'pillars' | 'helix' | 'asteroids';
Used by: spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.theme, spacelab.theme-customizer.

## ThemeGridStyle
Coordinate reference grid style drawn behind the scene (`'off'` disables grid rendering).
    export type ThemeGridStyle = 'cartesian' | 'polar' | 'dots' | 'hex' | 'off';
Used by: spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.theme, spacelab.theme-customizer.

## ThemeCounterStyle
Numeric readout and telemetry counter typography/color preset.
    export type ThemeCounterStyle = 'spacelab' | 'cyber' | 'apollo' | 'tactical' | 'aurora' | 'solar' | 'custom';
Used by: spacelab.theme, spacelab.theme-customizer.

## ThemeWindowStyle
Floating window frame and surface styling preset.
    export type ThemeWindowStyle = 'classic' | 'cyber' | 'apollo' | 'tactical' | 'aurora' | 'solar' | 'void' | 'crimson';
Used by: spacelab.theme, spacelab.theme-customizer.

## RenderThemeOptions
Presentation-only visual theme options controlling backdrop, stars, coordinate grids, window chrome, HUD effects, and shader seeds.
    export interface RenderThemeOptions {
      readonly backdropStyle?: ThemeBackdropStyle;
      readonly nebulaVisible?: boolean;
      readonly nebulaIntensity?: number;
      readonly starsVisible?: boolean;
      readonly starDensity?: number;
      readonly dustVisible?: boolean;
      readonly galaxiesVisible?: boolean;
      readonly animatedNebula?: boolean;
      readonly ringsVisible?: boolean;
      readonly asteroidBeltVisible?: boolean;
      readonly gridEnabled?: boolean;
      readonly gridStyle?: ThemeGridStyle;
      readonly gridSpacing?: number;
      readonly gridOpacity?: number;
      readonly gridLabels?: boolean;
      readonly gridColor?: string;
      readonly windowStyle?: ThemeWindowStyle;
      readonly windowColor?: string;
      /** Window background alpha, 0.4–1; text and controls remain fully visible. */
      readonly windowOpacity?: number;
      readonly counterTheme?: ThemeCounterStyle;
      readonly hudColor?: string;
      readonly hudScanlines?: boolean;
      readonly hudGlow?: boolean;
      readonly hudChamfer?: boolean;
      readonly compactHud?: boolean;
      readonly highQuality?: boolean;
      readonly worldSeed?: number;
      readonly shaderSeed?: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.theme.

## RenderSection
Ordered drawing pass section reported to a `RenderTimer` during `Renderer.draw`.
    export type RenderSection = 'background' | 'planet' | 'path' | 'ship' | 'overlay';
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## RenderTimer
Optional profiler hook, called after each section has been drawn.
    export interface RenderTimer { lap(section: RenderSection): void }
Used by: spacelab.profiler, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## ShaderPass
Built-in WebGL shader program pass identifier (`'scene'`, `'vector'`, or `'text'`).
    export type ShaderPass = 'scene' | 'vector' | 'text';
Used by: spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.vector-shaders.

## ShaderSources
Vertex and fragment GLSL shader source strings for a single `ShaderPass`.
    export interface ShaderSources { readonly vertex: string; readonly fragment: string }
Used by: spacelab.render-webgl.

## ShaderPack
`space-sim-shaders/1` data. Packs restyle built-in drawing roles, never module overlays.
    export interface ShaderPack {
      readonly format: 'space-sim-shaders/1';
      readonly name: string;
      readonly passes: Readonly<Record<ShaderPass, ShaderSources>>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.vector-shaders.

## ShaderControl
Live shader pack controller exposed on `RendererFeatures.shaders` for atomic runtime shader replacement.
    export interface ShaderControl {
      readonly pack: ShaderPack;
      /** Validates and compiles atomically; on failure it throws and the previous pack keeps drawing. */
      set(pack: ShaderPack): void;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## RendererFeatures
Optional features of a renderer instance. Applications discover them here, never by class.
    export interface RendererFeatures {
      readonly shaders?: ShaderControl;
      readonly effects?: ShaderEffects;
      /** Finish deferred raster work so profiler sections are attributed precisely. */
      readonly flush?: () => void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## Renderer
Viewport scene renderer instance bound to a single `HTMLCanvasElement`; frees all GPU/canvas resources in `dispose()`.
    export interface Renderer {
      readonly features?: RendererFeatures;
      draw(frame: RenderFrame, timer?: RenderTimer): void;
      dispose?(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## RendererCapabilities
Declared before construction and matched by every renderer the provider creates.
    export interface RendererCapabilities {
      /** Accepts ShaderPack data and exposes `features.shaders`. */
      readonly shaders: boolean;
      /** Draws `RenderFrame.overlays`. */
      readonly overlays: boolean;
      readonly effects?: boolean;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## RendererProvider
The `renderer` slot of a presentation profile. A stateless factory: each renderer owns everything it allocates (contexts, programs, buffers, textures) and frees it in dispose(), so a renderer may outlive the candidate whose provider created it.
    export interface RendererProvider {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly version: string;
      /** Short UI label, such as "WebGL" or "2D". */
      readonly label: string;
      readonly capabilities: RendererCapabilities;
      create(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, options?: { readonly shaders?: ShaderPack }): Renderer;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-split.

## AudioCue
Device-local flight cues; none of these commands changes simulation state.
    export type AudioCue = 'confirm' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'warp' | 'land' | 'crash' | 'splash';
Used by: builtin.audio, spacelab.audio-controls.

## AudioOutput
Browser-owned output. Creation is silent; resume() is called after a user gesture. Levels are normalized to 0–1. The host suspends hidden tabs and disposes replaced outputs.
    export interface AudioOutput {
      resume(): Promise<void>;
      suspend(): void;
      setVolume(level: number): void;
      setThrust(level: number): void;
      play(cue: AudioCue): void;
      dispose(): void;
    }
Used by: builtin.audio, spacelab.audio-controls.

## AudioProvider
Optional `audio` presentation slot. A stateless factory, independent of renderer lifetime.
    export interface AudioProvider {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly version: string;
      readonly label: string;
      create(): AudioOutput;
    }
Used by: builtin.audio, spacelab.audio-controls.

## OverlaySpace
`world`: system coordinates in metres (+Y up), projected by the frame's camera. `screen`: CSS pixels from the viewport's top-left corner (+Y down). Colors are `#RRGGBB` or `#RRGGBBAA`; widths are CSS pixels in both spaces.
    export type OverlaySpace = 'world' | 'screen';
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## OverlayPrimitive
Declarative 2D vector overlay primitive (`'line'`, `'circle'`, or `'text'`) drawn in `'world'` metres or `'screen'` CSS pixels.
    export type OverlayPrimitive =
      /** `fill` paints the closed outline instead of stroking it; the shape must be convex. */
      | { readonly kind: 'line'; readonly space: OverlaySpace; readonly points: readonly Vec[]; readonly color: string;
          readonly width?: number; readonly closed?: boolean; readonly fill?: boolean }
      /** Radius is metres in world space and CSS pixels in screen space. */
      | { readonly kind: 'circle'; readonly space: OverlaySpace; readonly center: Vec; readonly radius: number;
          readonly color: string; readonly width?: number; readonly fill?: boolean }
      | { readonly kind: 'text'; readonly space: OverlaySpace; readonly at: Vec; readonly text: string; readonly color: string;
          readonly align?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right' };
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.navball, spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.throttle-hud, spacelab.view-hud.

## PresentationShip
Detached presentation snapshot of the piloted vessel, combining body-relative `state` (`m`, `m/s`, `rad`) and system-coordinate `position` (`m`).
    export interface PresentationShip {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly bodyId: string;
      /** Interpolated presentation state relative to `bodyId` (metres, m/s, radians). */
      readonly state: State;
      readonly controls: Controls;
      /** Position in system coordinates (metres), as drawn. */
      readonly position: Vec;
      readonly fault?: string;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## PresentationBody
Detached presentation snapshot of the active celestial body in system coordinates (`radius` and `position` in `m`, `mu` in `m³/s²`, optional `spin` in `rad/s`).
    export interface PresentationBody {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      /** Reference radius (metres) and gravitational parameter (m³/s²). */
      readonly radius: number;
      readonly mu: number;
      /** Centre in system coordinates at the view time. */
      readonly position: Vec;
      /** Signed spin rate in rad/s (counter-clockwise positive); absent when the body does not rotate. */
      readonly spin?: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## PresentationSnapshot
A detached, frozen snapshot of what the pilot sees. Built at most once per frame.
    export interface PresentationSnapshot {
      /** Interpolated presentation time (seconds) and the last committed simulation tick. */
      readonly time: number;
      readonly tick: number;
      readonly paused: boolean;
      /** Wall seconds since the previous frame. Presentation only; never feeds the simulation. */
      readonly elapsed: number;
      readonly viewport: { readonly width: number; readonly height: number; readonly pixelRatio: number };
      readonly body: PresentationBody;
      readonly ship?: PresentationShip;
      readonly vehicle: Readonly<VehicleDefaults>;
      /** The active motion model provides analytic orbital diagnostics. */
      readonly analyticOrbits: boolean;
      /** Analytic elements of a flying ship's orbit, when `analyticOrbits`. */
      readonly orbit: Elements | null;
      /** Height above local ground or sea in metres (negative underwater). */
      readonly groundClearance: number | null;
      readonly prediction: { readonly status: GhostEnd | 'tracing' | 'none'; readonly samples: number; readonly last?: GhostSample };
      /** Optional local flight plan. Body-relative SI coordinates; a preview never changes live motion. */
      readonly maneuver?: ManeuverPreview;
      /** Shared-world rule namespaces at the last committed tick. */
      readonly moduleState: Readonly<Record<string, WorldRuleState>>;
    }
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.navball.

## ManeuverPreview
Read-only body-relative SI preview of the active maneuver node and planned trajectory (`time` and `burnSeconds` in `s`, `position` in `m`, `deltaVelocity` in `m/s`).
    export interface ManeuverPreview {
      /** Completed nodes remain in the editor journal but no longer draw burn targets. */
      readonly completed?: boolean;
      readonly nodes?: readonly { readonly id: string; readonly time: number; readonly position: Vec; readonly label: string; readonly selected: boolean; readonly completed?: boolean; readonly valid: boolean; readonly deltaVelocity?: Vec; readonly throttleBurnSeconds?: number | null }[];
      readonly time: number;
      readonly position: Vec;
      readonly deltaVelocity: Vec;
      readonly burnSeconds: number;
      /** Estimate at the current throttle setting, even with engines off. Null means no thrust. */
      readonly throttleBurnSeconds?: number | null;
      readonly throttle?: number;
      readonly remainingDeltaV?: number;
      readonly valid: boolean;
      readonly path: readonly GhostSample[];
    }
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, spacelab.maneuver-editor.

## PresentationView
Frozen per-frame presentation snapshot extended with the active viewport `Camera`, passed to `LayerHook` and `PanelHook`.
    export interface PresentationView extends PresentationSnapshot {
      /** The camera this frame is drawn with. */
      readonly camera: Camera;
    }
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.navball.

## CameraInput
Frozen per-frame presentation snapshot extended with the pilot's zoom `scale` (CSS pixels per metre), passed to `CameraHook.frame`.
    export interface CameraInput extends PresentationSnapshot {
      /** The pilot's zoom in CSS pixels per metre. */
      readonly scale: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## LayerHook
Hooks below are synchronous presentation code. Each receives a frozen snapshot and returns data the host validates. A thrown error or invalid result disables only that contribution (reported with module ID and hook); the simulation never observes it.
    export interface LayerHook {
      /** Local name; the contribution ID is `<module id>/<name>`. */
      readonly name: string;
      readonly label: string;
      /** Once per drawn frame. At most 256 primitives and 4,096 points per call. */
      draw(view: PresentationView): readonly OverlayPrimitive[];
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## PanelRow
Single telemetry row (`label`, `value`, and optional `unit`) returned by a `PanelHook`.
    export interface PanelRow { readonly label: string; readonly value: string; readonly unit?: string }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## PanelHook
Synchronous presentation telemetry panel hook returning up to 16 `PanelRow` entries from a frozen `PresentationView`.
    export interface PanelHook {
      readonly name: string;
      readonly title: string;
      /** At most four times per second. At most 16 rows; label/unit ≤ 32 and value ≤ 64 characters. */
      update(view: PresentationView): readonly PanelRow[];
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## CameraTarget
Camera center (system coordinates in metres) and optional counter-clockwise rotation (radians) returned by a `CameraHook`.
    export interface CameraTarget {
      /** System coordinates (metres). */
      readonly center: Vec;
      /** Target rotation in radians; omitted keeps the pilot's North up/Horizon up choice.
       * The host blends rotation changes and keeps the pilot's zoom. */
      readonly rotation?: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.camera-follow.

## CameraHook
Synchronous presentation camera mode hook computing a `CameraTarget` once per rendered frame from a frozen `CameraInput`.
    export interface CameraHook {
      readonly name: string;
      /** Camera button text while selected, such as "Lead camera". */
      readonly label: string;
      /** Once per frame while selected. */
      frame(input: CameraInput): CameraTarget;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## PresentationContribution
Resolved presentation hook contribution (`<moduleId>/<hook.name>`) bound to its owning module identity and version.
    export interface PresentationContribution<T> {
      readonly moduleId: string;
      readonly version: string;
      /** `<module id>/<hook name>`. */
      readonly id: string;
      readonly hook: T;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## LayerExtension
Resolved overlay `LayerHook` presentation contribution.
    export type LayerExtension = PresentationContribution<LayerHook>;
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## PanelExtension
Resolved telemetry `PanelHook` presentation contribution.
    export type PanelExtension = PresentationContribution<PanelHook>;
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## CameraExtension
Resolved `CameraHook` presentation contribution.
    export type CameraExtension = PresentationContribution<CameraHook>;
Used by: spacelab.camera-follow.

## PresentationFault
Diagnostic fault record emitted when a presentation `layer`, `panel`, or `camera` hook throws or returns invalid data.
    export interface PresentationFault {
      readonly moduleId: string;
      /** Contribution ID, such as `builtin.flight-vectors/velocity`. */
      readonly hook: string;
      readonly kind: 'layer' | 'panel' | 'camera';
      readonly message: string;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

=== game-api: profiler.ts [api-reference/game-api/profiler]
Declarations from profiler.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/profiler.ts.

## ProfileSection
A timed part of a frame: a render section or one of `physics`, `prediction`, `hud`.
    export type ProfileSection = RenderSection | 'physics' | 'prediction' | 'hud';
Used by: spacelab.profiler.

## ProfileStats
Distribution of one measurement over the retained frames, in wall-clock milliseconds. All zero when no frame was recorded.
    export interface ProfileStats {
      /** Mean. */ readonly avg: number;
      /** Median. */ readonly p50: number;
      /** 95th percentile. */ readonly p95: number;
      /** 99th percentile. */ readonly p99: number;
      /** Largest value. */ readonly max: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.profiler.

## ProfileSummary
Snapshot of the retained window of at most 600 frames. Treat it as read-only; it may be cached until the next frame ends.
    export interface ProfileSummary {
      /** Frames recorded since creation, not capped at the window size. */
      readonly frames: number;
      /** Frames per second implied by the mean frame interval; 0 with no data. */
      readonly fps: number;
      /** Wall time between frame starts, in ms. */
      readonly interval: ProfileStats;
      /** Game work per frame, in ms, from `begin` to the last `lap`. */
      readonly work: ProfileStats;
      /** Per-section time in ms. */
      readonly sections: Readonly<Record<ProfileSection,ProfileStats>>;
      /** Frames flagged as long work or hitches since creation. */
      readonly slowFrames: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.profiler.

## ProfileEvent
One bounded-log event. `t` is wall-clock seconds on the viewer's monotonic clock; extra fields are free-form detail.
    export interface ProfileEvent { readonly t: number; readonly type: string; readonly [detail: string]: unknown }
Used by: spacelab.profiler.

## FrameProfiler
Viewer-local wall-clock measurements; never feeds simulation or authoritative identity.
    export interface FrameProfiler extends RenderTimer, Disposable {
      /** When true each `lap` first calls the flush callback so deferred raster work is attributed to the section that queued it. Slower. */
      precise: boolean;
      /** Wall-clock date at creation. */
      readonly startedAt: Date;
      /** Most recent `slow-frame` event, or undefined. */
      readonly lastSlow: ProfileEvent | undefined;
      /** Starts a frame. `intervalMs` is the wall time since the previous frame started. */
      begin(intervalMs: number): void;
      /** Attributes the time since the previous lap (or `begin`) to `section`. */
      lap(section: ProfileSection): void;
      /** Ends the frame, stores it in the 600-frame ring and may log a `slow-frame` event. Call once per `begin`. */
      end(): void;
      /** Appends an event to the 1000-event log (oldest dropped) and returns it. After disposal it is returned but not stored. */
      event(type: string, detail?: Record<string,unknown>): ProfileEvent;
      /** Computes statistics for the retained frames on request; cached until the next frame ends. */
      summary(): ProfileSummary;
      /** Statistics of the per-frame sum of several sections, not the sum of their percentiles. */
      group(sections: readonly ProfileSection[]): ProfileStats;
      /** Oldest-first copy of the retained frames (milliseconds, rounded) for export. Allocates. */
      history(): Array<Record<string,number>>;
      /** The live bounded event log, oldest first. Do not mutate. */
      log(): readonly ProfileEvent[];
      /** Draws stacked per-section bars of recent frames into a 2D canvas, newest at the right. Does nothing after disposal. */
      drawGraph(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.profiler.

## ProfilerOptions
Optional tuning for a profiler. Every value is viewer-only and never affects simulation.
    export interface ProfilerOptions {
      /** Start in precise mode. Default false. */
      readonly precise?: boolean;
      /** Game work above this many ms flags a slow frame. Default 11. */
      readonly longWorkMs?: number;
      /** Frame interval in ms above which a hitch may be flagged. Default 25. */
      readonly hitchMs?: number;
      /** A hitch must also exceed this multiple of the typical interval. Default 2.5. */
      readonly hitchRatio?: number;
      /** Full height of the graph in ms. Default 33.4. */
      readonly graphMs?: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.profiler.

## ProfilerFactory
Creates independent profilers. The factory owns them: disposing it disposes every instance.
    export interface ProfilerFactory extends Disposable {
      /** `flush` is optional and is called before each lap in precise mode (for example to read back a canvas pixel). Throws after disposal. */
      create(flush?: () => void, options?: ProfilerOptions): FrameProfiler
    }
Used by: spacelab.profiler.

## PROFILERS
Service key for the frame-profiler provider (`diagnostics.profiler@1.0.0`).
    export const PROFILERS: ServiceKey<ProfilerFactory> = Object.freeze({slot:'diagnostics.profiler',version:'1.0.0'});
Used by: spacelab.profiler.
-> API reference index [api-reference]

=== game-api: render-math.ts [api-reference/game-api/render-math]
Declarations from render-math.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/render-math.ts.

## project
Project a single world-space point (metres, `+Y` up) into viewport screen coordinates (CSS pixels, `+Y` down).
    export function project(p: Vec, camera: Camera, width: number, height: number): Vec;
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## createProjector
Cache camera constants once per frame, shared by every body/path/ship vertex.
    export function createProjector(camera: Camera, width: number, height: number): (p: Vec) => Vec;
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## screenToWorld
Project a screen-space coordinate (CSS pixels) to world coordinates.
    export function screenToWorld(screenPos: Vec, camera: Camera, width: number, height: number): Vec;
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## dragCamera
Move the scene by a screen-space pointer displacement (CSS pixels).
    export function dragCamera(camera: Camera, delta: Vec): Camera;
Used by: spacelab.camera-follow.

## blendAngle
Interpolate along the shortest angular arc in radians by `fraction` clamped to `[0, 1]`, wrapped to `(-pi, pi]`.
    export function blendAngle(current: number, target: number, fraction: number): number;
Used by: spacelab.camera-follow.

## interpolateState
Presentation snapshot `alpha` of the way from one fixed physics step to the next, so motion stays smooth when 10-ms steps do not divide the display's frame interval. Visual only: physics must never read the result.
    export function interpolateState(from: State, to: State, alpha: number): State;
Used by: spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.spin-view.

## hermiteControls
Bézier control points of the cubic Hermite curve between two timed states. Exact end positions and velocities keep sparse quantized samples smooth at any zoom; the projection is affine, so world-space control points project directly.
    export function hermiteControls(a: GhostSample, b: GhostSample): readonly [Vec, Vec];
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## getPathGeometry
Compute and WeakMap-cache cumulative chord lengths (metres) and Hermite control points for a trajectory sample array.
    export function getPathGeometry(path: readonly GhostSample[]): PathGeometry;
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## pathFromShip
The drawn part of a path. Open paths drop samples the ship has passed and begin at the ship; `flown` is the chord length already behind it, which keeps the dashes world-fixed.
    export function pathFromShip(path: readonly GhostSample[], closed: boolean,
      ship: GhostSample): { readonly points: readonly GhostSample[]; readonly flown: number };
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## trajectoryMarkers
Restrained timed SOI markers and future body outlines. All snapshots are in the path's translating reference frame, so apply pathOrigin exactly once. Old providers can omit encounterBody. No renderer-side physics/ephemeris queries.
    export function trajectoryMarkers(frame: RenderFrame): OverlayPrimitive[];
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

=== game-api: rendering.ts [api-reference/game-api/rendering]

## VEHICLE_MARKER

## SceneFrame

      readonly time: number;
    }

## SceneRenderer
Presentation renderer capable of drawing flight and standalone scene frames to an owned canvas.
    export interface SceneRenderer extends Renderer {

## BodyPreview
Lightweight preview renderer for standalone planet or celestial body preview displays.
    export interface BodyPreview extends Disposable {

      readonly ready: boolean;
      /** Diagnostic error string if shader compilation or context acquisition failed. */
      readonly error?: string;
      /** Draw a preview of the specified body with optional shading fill factor. */

Used by: spacelab.planet-designer, spacelab.render-webgl.

## Render2DOptions
Configuration options passed when creating a 2D canvas or WebGL scene renderer.
    export interface Render2DOptions {
      /** Optional vehicle appearance catalog; falls back to generic marker when omitted. */
      readonly vehicles?: VehicleArtwork;
      /** Presentation resolution cap, 0.5–4; never alters physical simulation or body dimensions. */
      readonly maxPixelRatio?: number;
      /** Physical terrain surface query port for procedural body drawing. */
      readonly geometry?: RenderGeometry;
      /** Procedural backdrop and styling provider. */
      readonly theme?: ThemeArtwork;
      /** Explicit compatibility fallback body when a scene frame contains zero bodies. */
      readonly fallbackBody?: RenderBody;
    }
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.
