=== game-api: rules.ts [api-reference/game-api/rules]
Declarations from rules.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/rules.ts.

## DeterministicMath
Cross-engine deterministic floating-point math operations supplied to authoritative world rules.
    export interface DeterministicMath {
      sin(x: number): number;
      cos(x: number): number;
      atan2(y: number, x: number): number;
      hypot(x: number, y: number): number;
      exp(x: number): number;
      sqrt(x: number): number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## WorldRuleHook
Once per candidate tick, after all motion/SOI results. Each hook reads the same detached candidate ships/events and its own prior namespace. Commands apply in resolved hook order at the next control boundary. A shared failure aborts and pauses the whole tick; per-ship motion faults retain their existing behavior.
    export interface WorldRuleHook {
      readonly modelIdentity: string;
      readonly initialState: ModuleState;
      /** Nonzero uint32 seed. RNG authority is committed/rolled back with state. */
      readonly seed: number;
      evaluate(input: Readonly<{
        tick: number; time: number; seconds: number; ships: readonly Ship[];
        events: readonly WorldEvent[]; state: ModuleState;
        math: DeterministicMath; random: () => number;
      }>): Readonly<{ state: ModuleState; commands?: readonly RuleCommand[] }>;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## WorldRuleExtension
Resolved authoritative world-rule contribution bound to its owning module identity and version.
    export interface WorldRuleExtension {
      readonly moduleId: string;
      readonly version: string;
      readonly hook: WorldRuleHook;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## WorldRuleState
Persisted authoritative world-rule namespace combining module state and deterministic PRNG uint32 state.
    export interface WorldRuleState {
      readonly state: ModuleState;
      readonly randomState: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

=== game-api: services.ts [api-reference/game-api/services]
Declarations from services.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/services.ts.

## ProviderIdentity
Canonical identity and behavioral `modelIdentity` fingerprint of an authoritative motion service provider.
    export interface ProviderIdentity {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly version: string;
      /** All parameters affecting behavior; local identity, not a package fingerprint. */
      readonly modelIdentity: string;
    }
Used by: builtin.motion.

## OrbitalProvider
Analytic providers promise two-body propagation and conservative radius events. A provider without that verified contract sets analytic=false; ordinary motion remains usable.
    export interface OrbitalProvider extends ProviderIdentity {
      readonly analytic: boolean;
      elements(state: OrbitalState, mu: number): Elements;
      propagate(state: OrbitalState, mu: number, seconds: number, options?: SolverOptions): Propagation;
      timeToRadius(state: OrbitalState, mu: number, radius: number, direction: 'inbound' | 'outbound', elements?: Elements): number | null;
    }
Used by: builtin.motion.

## SurfaceSupport
Local terrain contact support at a polar angle (radians): query sample, outward support normals, and maximum slope angle in radians.
    export interface SurfaceSupport {
      readonly surface: SurfaceQuery;
      readonly normals: readonly Vec[];
      readonly maxSlopeRadians: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## SurfaceProvider
All geometry uses the public radial SurfaceProfile contract. Query, contact and generation must agree, including preset placement, water and prediction.
    export interface SurfaceProvider extends ProviderIdentity {
      generate(body: Body): SurfaceProfile;
      identity(body: Body): string;
      validate(body: Body, profile: SurfaceProfile): void;
      query(profile: SurfaceProfile, angle: number): SurfaceQuery;
      support(profile: SurfaceProfile, angle: number): SurfaceSupport;
      sweep(profile: SurfaceProfile, start: Vec, end: Vec, options?: SurfaceSweepOptions): SurfaceSweep;
      sweepGround(profile: SurfaceProfile, start: Vec, end: Vec, options?: SurfaceSweepOptions): SurfaceSweep;
    }
Used by: builtin.motion.

## BodyFrameProvider
Owns ephemerides, coordinate changes, SOI selection and preset placement. Returned systems are isolated per world; mutable derived ephemeris caches are permitted.
    export interface BodyFrameProvider extends ProviderIdentity {
      load(universe: UniverseDefinition, systemId: string, surfaces: SurfaceProvider): LoadedSystem;
    }
Used by: builtin.motion.

## RailsBinding
Tuple of `modelIdentity` strings (`dynamics`, `orbits`, `bodies`, `surfaces`) that a `RailsProvider` certifies for analytic sleep.
    export interface RailsBinding {
      readonly dynamics: string;
      readonly orbits: string;
      readonly bodies: string;
      readonly surfaces: string;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## RailsProvider
Sleep requires an explicit compatible binding and a conservative event horizon. 0 means unknown; Infinity is allowed only when no event is possible. The world additionally checks idle controls, contact and exact fixed-point eligibility. Evaluation uses the selected orbital provider and the context's guarded coastAt.
    export interface RailsProvider extends ProviderIdentity {
      readonly compatible: readonly RailsBinding[];
      horizon(system: LoadedSystem, body: ResolvedBody, state: OrbitalState, epoch: OrbitalState, time: number, top: number): number;
    }
Used by: builtin.motion.

## MotionServices
Group of motion services composed into simulation contexts: orbits, body frames, surfaces, and rails.
    export interface MotionServices {
      readonly orbits: OrbitalProvider;
      readonly bodies: BodyFrameProvider;
      readonly surfaces: SurfaceProvider;
      readonly rails: RailsProvider;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## RuleCommand
Authoritative command emitted by world rules to modify ship controls or throttle.
    export interface RuleCommand {
      readonly shipId: string;
      readonly controls?: Controls;
      readonly throttle?: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

=== game-api: session.ts [api-reference/game-api/session]
Declarations from session.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/session.ts.

## SessionPlayer
Connected or saved player roster entry in a flight session (`host` is true for the world authority).
    export interface SessionPlayer { readonly id: string; readonly name: string; readonly connected: boolean; readonly host?: boolean }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ChatLine
Chat message delivered across a flight session (`from` is the sender's player ID when known).
    export type ChatLine = { readonly from?: string; readonly name: string; readonly text: string };
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## SessionRead
What the flight loop needs from a game, whether this browser owns the world or follows someone else's.
    export interface SessionRead {
      readonly kind: 'local' | 'client';
      readonly world: WorldRead;
      readonly playerId: string;
      readonly paused: boolean;
      readonly pauseReason: string;
      readonly warp: number;
      readonly requestedWarp: number;
      readonly warpReason: string;
      /** True if this seat controls time warp (only the host can activate warp). */
      readonly canWarp: boolean;
      setWarp(factor: number): void;
      /** Presentation fraction of the next fixed step. */
      readonly alpha: number;
      /** Ship record before the latest fixed step, for interpolation. */
      previous(shipId: string): Ship | undefined;
      /** Fading body-relative display correction; zero for local worlds. */
      displayOffset(shipId: string): Vec;
      /** Advance by wall-clock seconds. Held controls apply to `shipId` only.
       * On a local normal-speed tick, beforeTick runs first (owned fleet commands),
       * then beforeStep may replace the selected ship controls. Neither runs on rails. */
      update(seconds: number, shipId: string | undefined, controls: Controls, beforeStep?: () => Controls, beforeTick?: () => void): WorldEvent[];
      /** Returns the new ship's id when it exists immediately (local worlds). */
      spawn(presetId: string, godMode: boolean, vehicleId?: string): string | undefined;
      respawn(shipId: string, presetId: string): void;
      /** Place an owned ship (throw, orbit and teleport cheats). Local worlds apply it now or
       * throw a SessionError; remote hosts check it and answer with the next snapshot or a notice. */
      teleport(shipId: string, bodyId: string, position: Vec, velocity: Vec, heading?: number): void;
      remove(shipId: string): void;
      setThrottle(shipId: string, throttle: number): void;
      setGodMode(shipId: string, enabled: boolean): void;
      setGodModeAll(enabled: boolean): void;
      /** A module command for an owned ship. Local worlds apply it now through the host's
       * checks (or throw a SessionError); remote results arrive with the next snapshot. */
      command(shipId: string, commandId: string, args?: Readonly<Record<string, number>>): void;
      players(): readonly SessionPlayer[];
      playerName(id: string): string | undefined;
      /** Say something to everyone in this world (just this pilot when playing alone). */
      chat(text: string): void;
      dataCommand(request: ModuleDataCommand): void;
      /** Every chat line in this world, this pilot's own included. */
      onchat: ((line: ChatLine) => void) | null;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.session-flight.

## SessionHandle
Disposable session handle combining `SessionRead` flight loop operations with deterministic cleanup.
    export interface SessionHandle extends SessionRead, Disposable {}
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## LocalSessionOptions
Options for starting an authoritative local session over a `WorldControl` instance.
    export interface LocalSessionOptions {
      readonly name: string; readonly token?: string; readonly hostId?: string; readonly players?: readonly SavedPlayer[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## JoinOptions
Options for joining a remote flight session over a `TextConnection`, including authority lock/fingerprint verification and reconnect token callbacks.
    export interface JoinOptions {
      readonly vehicles?: VehicleCatalog;
      readonly dataAdapters?: readonly ModuleDataExtension[];
      readonly createContext?: SimulationContextFactory; readonly name: string; readonly password?: string;
      /** May reserve a saved pilot asynchronously before sending the join request. */
      readonly token?: (worldId: string, hostId?: string) => string | undefined | Promise<string | undefined>;
      readonly saveToken?: (worldId: string, token: string, playerId: string) => void;
      /** Monotonic milliseconds, used only for network scheduling/interpolation. */
      readonly now?: () => number;
      readonly lock?: AuthoritativeLock | ModuleLock; readonly simulationFingerprint?: string;
      readonly rules?: readonly WorldRuleExtension[]; readonly forces?: readonly ForceExtension[]; readonly commands?: readonly CommandExtension[];
      /** Validate only against locally installed/enabled modules. A different server universe
       * may return its independently computed fingerprint; returning true alone retains the
       * local simulationFingerprint requirement. Never return the host's hash without recomputing it. */
      readonly verifyCandidate?: (candidate: {
        lock?: AuthoritativeLock | ModuleLock; fingerprint?: string; universe: UniverseDefinition; systemId: string;
      }) => { ok: boolean; reason?: string; simulationFingerprint?: string } | boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## LocalSessionControl

      readonly token: string; readonly hosting: boolean;

      spawnGuest(player: string, preset: string, godMode?: boolean): string;
      respawnGuest(player: string, ship: string, preset: string, godMode?: boolean): void;
      setGuestThrottle(player: string, ship: string, value: number): void;
      accept(connection: TextConnection): void;
      setPaused(paused: boolean, reason?: string): void; settle(): void;
      catchUp(seconds: number, ship: string | undefined): WorldEvent[];
      service(): void; snapshot(): string; roster(): readonly SavedPlayer[]; close(reason?: string): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.session-flight.

    export interface LocalSessionHandle extends SessionHandle { readonly local: LocalSessionControl }

## SessionGreeting

## FoundSession
Discovered server endpoint record with verified protocol compatibility.
    export interface FoundSession {
      /** Target server URL or connection address. */

      /** Human-readable session or world name. */
      readonly name: string;
      /** Current count of connected players. */
      readonly players: number;
      /** Maximum players accepted by the host. */

## LanScanOptions
Options controlling a concurrent discovery scan across candidate addresses.
    export interface LanScanOptions {
      /** Maximum number of simultaneous probes (1–64, default 32). */
      readonly concurrency?: number;
      /** Timeout per candidate probe in milliseconds (1–60000, default 800). */
      readonly timeoutMs?: number;
      /** Abort signal cancelling pending and future candidate probes. */
      readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
      /** Callback invoked as each compatible or incompatible server responds. */
      readonly onfound?: (server: FoundSession) => void;
      /** Progress callback reporting (probesCompleted, totalCandidates). */
      readonly onprogress?: (done: number, total: number) => void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.discovery-lan.

## LanDiscoveryOptions
Configuration options for a LAN discovery scanner instance.
    export interface LanDiscoveryOptions {
      /** Default probe timeout in milliseconds. */

      /** Default concurrency limit for candidate scanning. */
      readonly concurrency?: number;
      /** Maximum characters accepted in a discovery greeting before aborting (default 16384). */
      readonly maxGreetingLength?: number;
      /** Borrowed transport provider used to establish probe connections. */
      readonly transport: TransportProvider;
      /** Expected session protocol identifier (e.g. `space-sim-flight/1`). */
      readonly protocolId: string;
      /** Optional model identifier to check for exact world model compatibility. */
      readonly modelId?: string;
      /** Protocol adapter parsing the initial server text message into a SessionGreeting. */
      greeting(text: string): SessionGreeting | undefined;
    }
Used by: spacelab.discovery-lan.

## LanDiscovery
Active LAN discovery scanner instance.
    export interface LanDiscovery extends Disposable {

      scan(candidates: readonly string[], options?: LanScanOptions): Promise<FoundSession[]>;

=== game-api: shader-format.ts [api-reference/game-api/shader-format]
Declarations from shader-format.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/shader-format.ts.

## validateShaderDefinition
Validate, bound and freeze a shader pack definition against baseline defaults. Partial packs inherit built-in stages, never stages from a previously loaded custom pack. @param value Raw object representation of a shader pack. @param defaults Baseline defaults providing fallback stages for omitted passes. @returns Frozen, validated ShaderPack with resolved passes. @throws If format differs from 'space-sim-shaders/1', stages exceed 256 KiB, or fields are invalid.
    export function validateShaderDefinition(value: unknown, defaults: ShaderPack): ShaderPack;
Used by: spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.vector-shaders.

## parseShaderDefinition
Parse and validate a GLSL fragment file or JSON shader pack string. A .frag/.glsl file replaces just the scene fragment; JSON can replace all six stages. @param text Source file text (UTF-8, up to 1 MiB). @param filename Source filename used for extension detection and naming. @param defaults Baseline defaults providing fallback stages. @returns Frozen, validated ShaderPack with resolved passes. @throws If file exceeds 1 MiB or shader syntax validation fails.
    export function parseShaderDefinition(text: string, filename: string, defaults: ShaderPack): ShaderPack;
Used by: spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.vector-shaders.
-> API reference index [api-reference]

=== game-api: smart-camera.ts [api-reference/game-api/smart-camera]

## SmartCameraTarget
Viewer identities only: targets may be vehicles, celestial bodies or any other object.
    export interface SmartCameraTarget { readonly id: string; readonly name: string }

## SmartCameraCandidate
Candidate target with world-metre position for smart camera targeting searches.

## SmartCameraSearch
Search criteria describing camera center, drag velocity, rotation and priority candidate groups.
    export interface SmartCameraSearch<T extends SmartCameraCandidate> {
      readonly currentCenter: Vec;
      /** Drag-release velocity in CSS pixels per wall second. */
      readonly screenVelocity: Vec;
      readonly cameraRotation: number;
      /** CSS pixels per world metre. Groups are searched in priority order. */
      readonly scale: number;
      readonly groups: readonly (readonly T[])[];
      readonly currentFocusId?: string;
    }
Used by: spacelab.camera-smart.

## SmartCameraFrame
Presentation frame describing world-metre camera origin, target position, pan and elapsed wall time.
    export interface SmartCameraFrame {
      /** Frame origin and target position in the same world-metre coordinate system. */
      readonly origin: Vec;
      readonly targetPosition?: Vec;
      /** Camera displacement from origin, and world metres per wall second. */
      readonly pan: Vec;
      readonly velocity: Vec;
      readonly scale: number;
      readonly elapsed: number;
      readonly instant: boolean;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## SmartCameraStep
Borrowed storage, reused on the next update; follow requests the base camera's own follow mode.
    export interface SmartCameraStep { readonly pan: Vec; readonly velocity: Vec; readonly follow: boolean }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## SmartCameraControl
Per-view smart camera guidance controller tracking and guiding toward candidate targets.
    export interface SmartCameraControl<T extends SmartCameraTarget = SmartCameraTarget> extends Disposable {
      target: T | undefined;
      focus: T | undefined;
      start(target: T, followOnArrival?: boolean): void;
      /** Stop guided movement while retaining an already acquired focus. */
      stop(): void;
      clear(): void;
      /** Undefined means ordinary base-camera movement; a missing target releases focus. */
      update(frame: SmartCameraFrame): SmartCameraStep | undefined;
    }
Used by: spacelab.camera-follow, spacelab.camera-smart.

## SmartCameraFactory

    export interface SmartCameraFactory {

    }

=== game-api: state.ts [api-reference/game-api/state]
Declarations from state.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/state.ts.

## Vec
Immutable 2D Cartesian vector (SI metres, `m/s`, or `m/s²` in world/body frames with `+Y` up, or CSS pixels in screen space with `+Y` down).
    export interface Vec { readonly x: number; readonly y: number }
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, builtin.navball, spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.camera-follow, spacelab.camera-smart, spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.maneuver-burn, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.session-flight, spacelab.spin-view, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## BodyRotation
Sidereal spin of a body: its terrain, oceans and anything resting on them turn with it.
    export interface BodyRotation {
      /** Seconds per turn. */
      readonly period: number;
      /** -1 turns clockwise, the same sense as default orbits; 1 counter-clockwise. */
      readonly direction: 1 | -1;
      /** Angle of the body-fixed frame at time 0, radians. */
      readonly phase: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## Body
Minimal authoritative celestial body parameters for motion and surface contact: reference `radius` in `m` and gravitational parameter `mu` (`GM`) in `m³/s²`.
    export interface Body {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly radius: number;
      readonly mu: number;
      readonly surface?: SurfaceDefinition;
      readonly rotation?: BodyRotation;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.spin-view.

## State
A flying state is in the body-centred inertial frame. On a rotating body a landed or crashed state is in the body-fixed frame instead: it rests with constant position and heading.
    export interface State {
      readonly moduleState?: Readonly<Record<string, ModuleState>>;
      readonly time: number;
      readonly position: Vec;
      readonly velocity: Vec;
      readonly heading: number;
      readonly throttle: number;
      readonly status: 'landed' | 'flying' | 'crashed';
      readonly revision: number;
      readonly godMode?: boolean;
      readonly coast?: Coast;
      readonly dynamics?: 'kepler' | 'numerical';
      readonly solver?: SolverDiagnostics;
      /** Terrain event epoch lies within this completed scheduler step. */
      readonly contact?: SurfaceContactEvent;
      readonly water?: 'submerged' | 'floating';
      /** Split drift vertex retained for prediction of a gentle sea entry. */
      readonly waterEntry?: GhostSample;
    }
Used by: spacelab.audio-controls, spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.flight-plans, spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.maneuver-burn, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.spin-view, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## SurfaceContactEvent
Terrain or sea contact event recorded during a step, with simulation `time` in `s`, step `fraction` in `[0, 1]`, and body-relative `impactVelocity` in `m/s`.
    export interface SurfaceContactEvent {
      readonly material?: 'rock' | 'water';
      readonly time: number;
      readonly fraction: number;
      readonly surfaceIdentity: string;
      readonly impactVelocity: Vec;
      readonly diagnostics: SurfaceSweepDiagnostics;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## Coast
Exact Keplerian coasting epoch (`epochSeconds` in `s`, body-centered inertial `position` in `m` and `velocity` in `m/s`, `mu` in `m³/s²`) from which unpowered motion is evaluated without drift.
    export interface Coast {
      readonly modelIdentity?: string;
      readonly bodyId: string;
      readonly epochSeconds: number;
      readonly position: Vec;
      readonly velocity: Vec;
      readonly mu: number;
      readonly revision: number;
      readonly surfaceRadius: number;
      readonly surfaceIdentity: string;
      /** Absent for the circular preview contact model, even on configured bodies. */
      readonly contactSurfaceIdentity?: string;
    }
Used by: spacelab.fleet-view, spacelab.maneuver-editor.

## Controls
Held pilot flight control inputs: `turn` rate in `[-1, 1]` (positive turns counter-clockwise), `throttle` rate in `[-1, 1]`, and `engine` ignition boolean.
    export interface Controls { readonly turn: number; readonly throttle: number; readonly engine: boolean }
Used by: spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.flight-split, spacelab.maneuver-burn, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.session-flight.

## Preset
Built-in starting spawn preset identifier (`'pad'`, `'planet-orbit'`, or `'moon-orbit'`).
    export type Preset = 'pad' | 'planet-orbit' | 'moon-orbit';
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## Sample
Timed 2D position sample with simulation `time` in seconds and `position` in metres.
    export interface Sample { readonly time: number; readonly position: Vec }
Used by: spacelab.flight-prediction.

## GhostSample
Trajectory forecast sample with simulation `time` (`s`), `position` (`m`), and `velocity` (`m/s`) in the translating non-rotating frame of `frameId ?? bodyId`.
    export interface GhostSample extends Sample {
      readonly velocity: Vec;
      /** Segment ending here is a verified numerical drift, not a conic/Hermite arc. */
      readonly linear?: boolean;
      readonly contactMaterial?: 'water';
      /** Active gravitational body. Multi-body forecasts express position/velocity in
       * frameId's translating, nonrotating frame at this sample's time. */
      readonly bodyId?: string;
      readonly frameId?: string;
      /** First sample in a new SOI; continuous position, velocity and time. */
      readonly encounter?: { readonly from: string; readonly to: string };
      /** Destination body at this SOI handoff time, in the same translating frame as
       * position. Optional display snapshot, never current world coordinates. The
       * reference radius is not a terrain/contact boundary. */
      readonly encounterBody?: { readonly position: Vec; readonly radius: number; readonly name: string };
    }
Used by: builtin.maneuver-planner, spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## GhostEnd
Why an orbital ghost projection pass ended.
    export type GhostEnd = 'closed' | 'impact' | 'escape' | 'open' | 'grounded' | 'floating' | 'failed' | 'horizon';
Used by: spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## SurfaceDefinition
Deterministic radial terrain and sea generation recipe in SI metres and radians (`generatorVersion: 1`).
    export interface SurfaceDefinition {

      readonly maxReliefMetres: number;

      readonly pads?: readonly SurfacePadDefinition[];
    }

## SurfaceBounds
Conservative radial bounds (`minRadius`/`maxRadius` in `m`), radial derivative (`m/rad`), and slope tangent over a terrain profile or interval.
    export interface SurfaceBounds {
      readonly minRadius: number;
      readonly maxRadius: number;
      readonly maxAbsDerivative: number;
      readonly maxSlope: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## SurfaceQuery
Point evaluation of a `SurfaceProfile` at a body-fixed polar angle: radii, elevation, and water depth in metres, unit outward normals, and contact material.
    export interface SurfaceQuery {
      readonly groundRadius: number;
      readonly firstContactRadius: number;
      readonly waterDepth: number;
      readonly elevation: number;
      readonly groundNormal: Readonly<{ x: number; y: number }>;
      readonly normal: Readonly<{ x: number; y: number }>;
      readonly intervalIndex: number;
      readonly material: 'rock' | 'water';
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## SurfaceFeature
Authored or procedural radial terrain feature metadata (mountain range or crater) with dimensions in metres and polar angle in radians.
    export type SurfaceFeature = Readonly<{
      id: string;
      kind: 'mountain-range';
      angleRadians: number;
      widthMetres: number;
      heightMetres: number;
    } | {
      id: string;
      kind: 'crater';
      angleRadians: number;
      bowlRadiusMetres: number;
      rimWidthMetres: number;
      centralPeakWidthMetres: number;
      depthMetres: number;
      rimHeightMetres: number;
      centralPeakHeightMetres: number;
    }>;
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## SurfaceProfile
Private Float64 storage; reads/copies cannot mutate authoritative geometry. No cache or browser globals. Callers own profile lifetimes.
    export interface SurfaceProfile {
      readonly identity: string;
      readonly referenceRadius: number;
      readonly orientationRadians: number;
      readonly sampleCount: number;
      readonly seaRadiusMetres?: number;
      /** Exact polar-linear sea crossings, wrapped to [0, 2*pi). */
      readonly coastAngles: readonly number[];
      readonly hasWater: boolean;
      readonly bounds: SurfaceBounds;
      readonly features: readonly SurfaceFeature[];
      readonly pads: readonly SurfacePadDefinition[];
      /** Elevations plus three interval arrays; excludes JS objects and identity text. */
      readonly storageBytes: number;
      sampleElevation(index: number): number;
      intervalBounds(index: number): SurfaceBounds;
      copyElevations(): Float64Array;
    }
Used by: spacelab.flight-prediction, spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl.

## SurfaceSweepOptions
Geometric tolerance (`m`) and work budget options for continuous chord-vs-terrain collision sweeps.
    export interface SurfaceSweepOptions {
      /** Absolute geometric tolerance in metres; defaults to at least 1e-7 m. */
      readonly toleranceMetres?: number;
      /** Interval visits plus clearance evaluations, including root refinement. */
      readonly maxWork?: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## SurfaceSweepDiagnostics
Work and tolerance accounting returned with a `SurfaceSweep` result.
    export interface SurfaceSweepDiagnostics {
      readonly toleranceMetres: number;
      readonly intervals: number;
      readonly evaluations: number;
      readonly work: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## SurfaceSweep
Outcome of sweeping a body-fixed linear segment from `start` to `end` against a `SurfaceProfile`: `'clear'`, `'hit'` (with contact fraction and query), or `'failed'`.
    export type SurfaceSweep =
      | (SweepResult & { readonly status: 'clear' })
      | (SweepResult & { readonly status: 'hit'; readonly fraction: number;
        /** A bracket on the drift fraction, not a time-of-flight solution. */
        readonly fractionBracket: readonly [number, number];
        /** On the drift chord, within the reported tolerance of ground. */
        readonly position: Vec; readonly surface: SurfaceQuery; readonly clearanceMetres: number;
        readonly kind: 'crossing' | 'touching' })
      | (SweepResult & { readonly status: 'failed';
        readonly reason: 'work-limit' | 'precision' | 'initial-penetration';
        /** Only the prefix through this fraction was verified; do not accept the endpoint. */
        readonly verifiedFraction: number });
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

=== game-api: tab-transport.ts [api-reference/game-api/tab-transport]

## TabChannel
Message channel abstraction for intra-browser communication (e.g. `BroadcastChannel`).
    export interface TabChannel {
      /** Post a message object across all listeners on this channel name. */
      postMessage(message: unknown): void;
      /** Close and release this channel instance. */
      close(): void;
      /** Handler receiving incoming message events. */
      onmessage: ((event: { readonly data: unknown }) => void) | null;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-tab.

## TabLocks
Web Locks API abstraction for leader election and mutual exclusion between browser tabs.
    export interface TabLocks {
      /** Request an exclusive or shared lock by name. */
      request(name: string, options: { readonly signal?: AbortSignal }, callback: () => Promise<void>): Promise<unknown>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-tab.

## TabNetworkOptions
Options for configuring a scoped same-origin tab network instance.
    export interface TabNetworkOptions {
      /** Namespace or game scope partitioning BroadcastChannels and Web Locks. */
      readonly scope: string;
      /** Session protocol identifier (e.g. `space-sim-flight/1`). */
      readonly protocolId: string;
      /** Optional channel factory. Defaults to native `BroadcastChannel` in browser environments. */
      readonly channel?: (name: string) => TabChannel;
      /** Optional lock adapter. Defaults to `navigator.locks`. Null disables Web Locks. */
      readonly locks?: TabLocks | null;
      /** Heartbeat interval in milliseconds when Web Locks are unavailable. */
      readonly beatMs?: number;
      /** Inactivity timeout in milliseconds before considering an idle peer disconnected. */
      readonly timeoutMs?: number;
      /** Maximum wait in milliseconds for a host tab to answer a connection handshake. */
      readonly connectTimeoutMs?: number;
      /** Maximum message text length in UTF-16 code units. */
      readonly maxTextLength?: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.net-tab.

      readonly maxPlayers: number;

## TabNetwork
Tab transport network instance providing scoped peer-to-peer connections and discovery across browser tabs.
    export interface TabNetwork extends TransportProvider, Disposable {

      readonly scope: string;
      /** Session protocol identifier. */
      readonly protocolId: string;

=== game-api: theme.ts [api-reference/game-api/theme]

## ThemeSettings
Presentation theme settings specifying backdrop, celestial grid, HUD and window styling.
    export interface ThemeSettings extends RenderThemeOptions {
      /** Visual style for the procedural backdrop (e.g. 'spacelab', 'nebula', 'carina', 'void'). */
      backdropStyle: ThemeBackdropStyle;
      /** Whether procedural nebulae are rendered in the background. */
      nebulaVisible: boolean;
      /** Relative brightness and opacity of procedural nebulae (0.0 to 2.0). */
      nebulaIntensity: number;
      /** Whether background star layers are rendered. */
      starsVisible: boolean;
      /** Multiplier for procedural background star count (0.5 to 2.0). */
      starDensity: number;
      /** Whether interstellar dust clouds are visible. */
      dustVisible: boolean;
      /** Whether distant deep-space galaxies are visible. */
      galaxiesVisible: boolean;
      /** Whether procedural nebulae animate slowly over presentation time. */
      animatedNebula: boolean;
      /** Randomization seed for background generation; 0 uses the universe seed. */
      shaderSeed: number;

      /** Whether the coordinate / navigation grid is displayed. */
      gridEnabled: boolean;
      /** Grid projection style ('cartesian', 'polar', 'dots', 'hex', 'off'). */
      gridStyle: ThemeGridStyle;
      /** Grid spacing in CSS pixels (e.g. 80). */
      gridSpacing: number;
      /** Visual opacity of grid lines and markers (0.1 to 1.0). */
      gridOpacity: number;
      /** Whether coordinate values and distance labels are displayed on grid lines. */
      gridLabels: boolean;
      /** Optional custom hex color string for the grid (e.g. '#38bdf8'). */
      gridColor?: string;

      /** Visual style for digital counters and telemetry ('spacelab', 'cyber', 'apollo', etc.). */
      counterTheme: ThemeCounterStyle;
      /** Custom phosphor / telemetry accent color (e.g. '#00f0ff'). */
      hudColor?: string;
      /** Window framing style ('classic', 'cyber', 'apollo', 'tactical', etc.). */
      windowStyle?: ThemeWindowStyle;
      /** Opacity for translucent UI windows and panels (0.4 to 1.0). */
      windowOpacity: number;
      /** Custom window header and border accent color. */
      windowColor?: string;
      /** Whether retro CRT scanlines overlay the flight display. */
      hudScanlines: boolean;
      /** Whether phosphor HUD elements display a diffuse glow. */
      hudGlow: boolean;
      /** Whether HUD instruments and telemetry panels have chamfered corners. */
      hudChamfer: boolean;
      /** Whether HUD widgets collapse into a compact layout for small viewports. */
      compactHud: boolean;
      /** Whether full-resolution procedural textures and anti-aliasing are enabled. */
      highQuality: boolean;
    }

=== game-api: transfers.ts [api-reference/game-api/transfers]
Declarations from transfers.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/transfers.ts.

## TransferRequest
Transfer window search request specifying ship, destination body, target altitudes in SI metres, and duration limits.
    export interface TransferRequest {
     readonly shipId: string; readonly destinationId: string;
     /** SI metres above the destination's reference radius. Equal values select a circle. */
     readonly periapsisAltitude: number; readonly apoapsisAltitude: number;
     /** Optional total search horizon from the source epoch, in SI seconds. */
     readonly maxFlightSeconds?: number;
     /** Staged routes park in a high orbit beside the neighbouring moon when true or
      * omitted; false keeps low parking near each stop body. Honoured when the provider
      * declares parkingOrbits. */
     readonly highParking?: boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.transfer-planner.

## TransferOption
Evaluated transfer route candidate providing flight plan nodes, burn times, delta-v breakdown in m/s, and route structure.
    export interface TransferOption {
     readonly kind: 'efficient' | 'faster'; readonly plan: FlightPlan;
     readonly waitSeconds: number; readonly travelSeconds: number;
     readonly deltaV: number; readonly departureDeltaV: number; readonly arrivalDeltaV: number;
     readonly departureBurnSeconds: number; readonly arrivalBurnSeconds: number;
     readonly warning: string;
     /** Optional whole-route summary. IDs resolve through the same world hierarchy as the picker. */
     readonly route?: { readonly bodyIds:readonly string[];readonly stops:readonly string[];readonly maxFlightSeconds:number;readonly finiteValidated:boolean;readonly burns?:readonly {readonly nodeId:string;readonly deltaV:number;readonly seconds:number}[] };
    }
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## TransferProgress
Intermediate transfer search progress describing current phase, candidate counts, completion fraction, and best options so far.
    export interface TransferProgress {
     /** Optional explanation of the leg or whole-route validation currently running. */
     readonly phase?: string;
     readonly tested: number; readonly total: number; readonly accepted: number;
     /** Optional whole-search completion estimate in [0, 1], nondecreasing for one search.
      * tested/total may describe only the current leg or phase; hosts show progress as
      * indeterminate when this is absent. */
     readonly fraction?: number;
     /** Fully validated best options so far; optional for existing providers.
      * A new array signals changed results. Search may continue after publication. */
     readonly options?: readonly TransferOption[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## TransferResult
Final result of a bounded transfer search returning discovered options and an explanation if empty.
    export interface TransferResult {
     /** Fully validated whole-route alternatives, including those dominated by the displayed options. */
     readonly compared?: {readonly direct:number;readonly staged:number};
     readonly options: readonly TransferOption[]; readonly reason: string }
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## TransferRelationship
Inert bounded search; each next() performs one bounded candidate operation or shared forecast yield. return() cancels owned forecasts/contexts. No controls or live stepping.
    export type TransferRelationship = 'same-body' | 'child' | 'parent' | 'sibling' | 'other';
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## TransferPlanner
Generator-based bounded orbital transfer solver evaluating same-body and interbody trajectories (`flight.transfers@1.0.0`).
    export interface TransferPlanner {
     /** Bind the host-selected controller without a dependency on a higher module tier. */
     withBurns?(burns:ManeuverBurnFactory):TransferPlanner;
     /** Optional solver coverage for destination browsing. Absent: the search explains
      * suitability. A relationship being listed is not a feasibility guarantee. */
     readonly relationships?: readonly TransferRelationship[];
     /** Optional bounds for an explicit mission-duration input. */
     readonly maxFlightSeconds?:number;
     readonly defaultFlightSeconds?:number;
     /** True when search honours TransferRequest.highParking for intermediate stops. */
     readonly parkingOrbits?:boolean;
     search(world: WorldRead, request: TransferRequest): Generator<TransferProgress, TransferResult>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

## TRANSFERS
Service key for the orbital transfer planner (`flight.transfers@1.0.0`).
    export const TRANSFERS: ServiceKey<TransferPlanner> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'flight.transfers', version: '1.0.0' });
Used by: spacelab.maneuver-editor, spacelab.transfer-planner.

=== game-api: tutorials.ts [api-reference/game-api/tutorials]
Declarations from tutorials.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/tutorials.ts.

## TutorialValues
Read-only scalar telemetry map observed by tutorial runners (e.g. `flying`, `landed`, `coasting`, `safe`, `bound`, `bodyId`, `clearance` in metres, `radius` in metres, `radiusRatio`, `speed` in m/s, `periapsis` and `apoapsis` in metres, `periapsisRatio` and `apoapsisRatio`, `eccentricity`, `energy` in J/kg, `angularMomentum` in m²/s, `period` in seconds, `nodeCount`, `nodeDv` in m/s, and `alarmCount`). Never writes simulation state.
    export type TutorialValues = Readonly<Record<string, number | string | boolean>>;
Used by: spacelab.tutorial-runner.

## TutorialObservation
Detached read-only observation snapshot passed to a tutorial runner at start and on each update. Tutorials are read-only observers: they read scalar telemetry and never mutate simulation state.
    export interface TutorialObservation {
      /** Session, target and revision/teleport continuity. Changing it invalidates an attempt. */
      readonly identity: object;
      /** Monotonic simulation time in seconds. Rewinds (`time < lastTime`) or non-finite values interrupt the active attempt. */
      readonly time: number;
      /** Whether the simulation is currently paused; paused frames do not advance timed holds. */
      readonly paused: boolean;
      /** Read-only scalar telemetry fields evaluated against step conditions. */
      readonly values: TutorialValues;
    }
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

## TutorialCondition
Single scalar predicate evaluated by a `TutorialRunner` against `TutorialObservation.values` and the lesson-start baseline. Non-finite numbers or missing fields evaluate to `false`; `'ratio-gte'` and `'ratio-lte'` require a finite positive baseline.
    export interface TutorialCondition {
      /** Name of the scalar key in `TutorialValues` to inspect. */
      readonly field: string;
      /** Comparison operator: value threshold (`'gte'`, `'lte'`, `'eq'`), change from baseline (`'changed'`), or ratio to baseline (`'ratio-gte'`, `'ratio-lte'`). */
      readonly operator: 'gte' | 'lte' | 'eq' | 'changed' | 'ratio-gte' | 'ratio-lte';
      /** Target threshold or exact match value (omitted for `'changed'`). */
      readonly value?: number | string | boolean;
    }
Used by: spacelab.orbital-lessons, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

## TutorialStep
One sequential objective within a `TutorialLesson`: either simulation-checked `conditions` (up to 32) or a multiple-choice `question`, never both.
    export interface TutorialStep {
      /** Short step heading shown in the objective banner and HUD button. */
      readonly title: string;
      /** Actionable player instruction explaining what to fly, observe or answer. */
      readonly instruction: string;
      /** Optional guidance revealed when the learner opens the hint disclosure. */
      readonly hint: string;
      /** Read-only scalar predicates (1 to 32) that must all hold simultaneously. Mutually exclusive with `question`. */
      readonly conditions?: readonly TutorialCondition[];
      /** UI/planning objectives may complete while paused; they cannot have a timed hold. */
      readonly allowPaused?: boolean;
      /** Consecutive simulation seconds; paused frames never accrue credit. */
      readonly holdSeconds?: number;
      /** Multiple-choice knowledge check (mutually exclusive with `conditions`); `correct` is the 0-based index in `choices`. */
      readonly question?: { readonly choices: readonly string[]; readonly correct: number; readonly explanation: string };
    }
Used by: spacelab.orbital-lessons.

## TutorialLesson
Read-only definition of a multi-step tutorial lesson (1 to 64 steps). Versioned so changes to completion criteria invalidate stale completion records (`<id>@<version>`).
    export interface TutorialLesson {
      /** Stable lesson identifier within a course. */
      readonly id: string;
      /** Positive integer version encoded in persisted completion keys (`<id>@<version>`). */
      readonly version: number;
      /** Human-readable lesson title. */
      readonly title: string;
      /** Difficulty tier used to group lessons in the selector. */
      readonly level: 'First flights' | 'Orbital skills' | 'Advanced';
      /** Introductory summary shown above the objective. */
      readonly introduction: string;
      /** Expandable physics and orbital mechanics notes ("Go deeper"). */
      readonly deeper: string;
      /** Ordered sequence of 1 to 64 checked objectives or knowledge questions. */
      readonly steps: readonly TutorialStep[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.orbital-lessons, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

## TutorialRunner
Stateful read-only state machine evaluating a `TutorialLesson` against `TutorialObservation` snapshots and persisting versioned completion keys (`<lesson.id>@<lesson.version>`, up to 256 entries). Never mutates simulation state, DOM nodes or timers. Caller owns and disposes the runner.
    export interface TutorialRunner extends Disposable {
      /** Currently active lesson, or `undefined` when idle or stopped. */
      readonly lesson: TutorialLesson | undefined;
      /** Zero-based index of the current step in `lesson.steps`. */
      readonly step: number;
      /** `true` once all steps of `lesson` have been completed. */
      readonly finished: boolean;
      /** `true` if `observation.identity` changed or `observation.time` rewound or became non-finite during an active lesson. */
      readonly interrupted: boolean;
      /** Latest learner-facing status or explanation message. */
      readonly feedback: string;
      /** Read-only set of completed `<lesson.id>@<lesson.version>` keys loaded from storage and updated on lesson completion. */
      readonly completed: ReadonlySet<string>;
      /**
       * Validates `lesson` (1–64 steps, each with either 1–32 conditions or a valid question, non-negative finite `holdSeconds`,
       * and no `holdSeconds > 0` when `allowPaused` is set) and starts tracking from `observation` as the baseline.
       * Throws if disposed or if the lesson or observation time is invalid.
       */
      start(lesson: TutorialLesson, observation: TutorialObservation): void;
      /**
       * Evaluates the current step's conditions against `observation`. Interrupts the attempt if `identity` changed or simulation
       * `time` decreased. Advances the step once conditions hold for `holdSeconds` consecutive unpaused simulation seconds.
       */
      update(observation: TutorialObservation): void;
      /**
       * Submits a 0-based `choice` index for the current step's knowledge question.
       * Returns `true` and advances the step when `choice === question.correct`; returns `false` otherwise or when inactive.
       */
      answer(choice: number): boolean;
      /** Clears the active lesson, interruption state and hold timer while preserving `completed` progress. */
      stop(): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.tutorial-runner.

## TutorialRunnerFactory
Stateless factory creating independent `TutorialRunner` state machines (`learning.runner@1.0.0`).
    export interface TutorialRunnerFactory {
      /**
       * Creates a runner backed by optional `storage` under `storageKey` (recommended format: `<courseId>/v<courseVersion>/<learnerKey>`).
       * Storage read/write failures fall back silently to in-memory tracking.
       */
      create(options: { readonly storage?: ThemePreferences; readonly storageKey: string }): TutorialRunner;
    }
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

## TUTORIAL_RUNNERS
Service key for the reusable tutorial runner factory (`learning.runner@1.0.0`).
    export const TUTORIAL_RUNNERS: ServiceKey<TutorialRunnerFactory> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'learning.runner', version: '1.0.0' });
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.tutorial-runner.

## AcademyPractice
Declarative practice ship placement requested only by an explicit learner button action. Ratios are measured from the body's reference radius; no world physics is changed.
    export interface AcademyPractice {
      /** Placement archetype: launch pad respawn (`'pad'`), bound orbit (`'circle'`, `'ellipse'`), hyperbolic lunar approach (`'approach'`), or moon transfer (`'encounter'`). */
      readonly kind: 'pad' | 'circle' | 'ellipse' | 'approach' | 'encounter';
      /** Target celestial body identifier (e.g. `'planet'` for Primus or `'moon'` for Parvus). */
      readonly bodyId: string;
      /** Periapsis or low-side radius expressed as a multiple of `body.radius`. */
      readonly low: number;
      /** Apoapsis or high-side radius expressed as a multiple of `body.radius`. */
      readonly high: number;
      /** Optional starting apsis for elliptical placements (`'periapsis'` or `'apoapsis'`). */
      readonly at?: 'periapsis' | 'apoapsis';
    }
Used by: spacelab.orbital-lessons.

## AcademyTarget
Optional geometric teaching-guide ellipse ratios (`low` and `high` as multiples of `body.radius`). Purely illustrative: never predicts burns, steers the ship or alters simulation state.
    export interface AcademyTarget {
      /** Periapsis radius of the suggested guide orbit as a multiple of `body.radius`. */
      readonly low: number;
      /** Apoapsis radius of the suggested guide orbit as a multiple of `body.radius`. */
      readonly high: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

## OrbitalLesson
An `OrbitalCourse` lesson pairing a `TutorialLesson` with an explicit `AcademyPractice` setup and optional `AcademyTarget` guide ellipse.
    export interface OrbitalLesson extends TutorialLesson {
      /** Practice setup loaded only when the learner explicitly clicks "Load practice setup" or "Restart practice". */
      readonly practice: AcademyPractice;
      /** Optional suggested target orbit ratios (`low` and `high` in multiples of `body.radius`) drawn as a cyan teaching guide. */
      readonly target?: AcademyTarget;
    }
Used by: spacelab.orbital-lessons.

## OrbitalCourse
Immutable orbital mechanics curriculum (`learning.orbital-course@1.0.0`) consumed by `learning.academy`.
    export interface OrbitalCourse {
      /** Stable course identifier used in progress storage keys. */
      readonly id: string;
      /** Positive integer course version used to namespace learner progress (`<id>/v<version>/<learnerKey>`). */
      readonly version: number;
      /** Display title for the Academy window. */
      readonly title: string;
      /** Ordered catalogue of beginner, orbital-skills and advanced lessons. */
      readonly lessons: readonly OrbitalLesson[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.orbital-lessons.

## ORBITAL_COURSE
Service key for the orbital mechanics course data (`learning.orbital-course@1.0.0`).
    export const ORBITAL_COURSE: ServiceKey<OrbitalCourse> = Object.freeze({ slot: 'learning.orbital-course', version: '1.0.0' });
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui, spacelab.orbital-lessons.

## AcademyFlight
Detached committed flight state, SI and body-relative inertial coordinates. No mutable session/world reference crosses the learning boundary. Tutorials read this snapshot to derive `TutorialObservation` values and never write back to the world.
    export interface AcademyFlight {
      /** Opaque continuity token for the controlled session, ship and teleport/respawn epoch. */
      readonly identity: object;
      /** Committed simulation time in seconds. */
      readonly time: number;
      /** Whether the flight session is currently paused. */
      readonly paused: boolean;
      /** Detached ship state in body-relative SI coordinates (metres, m/s, radians). */
      readonly state: State;
      /** Whether the ship's main engine is currently firing. */
      readonly engine: boolean;
      /** Active gravitating body parameters in SI units (`radius`, `surfaceTop` and `soi` in metres; `mu` in m³/s²). */
      readonly body: { readonly id: string; readonly name: string; readonly radius: number; readonly mu: number;
        readonly surfaceTop: number; readonly soi: number };
      /** Analytic two-body Keplerian elements when `analytic` is `true` and the ship is flying, or `null`. */
      readonly orbit: Elements | null;
      /** Height above terrain or sea level in metres, or `null` when unavailable. */
      readonly clearance: number | null;
      /** Whether the active motion model provides analytic two-body orbital elements. */
      readonly analytic: boolean;
      /** Current trajectory prediction end classification (e.g. `'closed'`, `'impact'`, `'escape'`). */
      readonly prediction: string;
      /** Count of valid planned maneuver nodes for the selected ship. */
      readonly plannedNodes: number;
      /** Total planned delta-v magnitude in m/s across valid maneuver nodes. */
      readonly plannedDeltaV: number;
      /** Count of pending (unhandled) orbital alarms. */
      readonly alarms: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

## AcademyPanelOptions
Host adapters supplied when mounting an `AcademyPanel`. Keeps the Academy UI decoupled from mutable sessions: `read()` returns detached snapshots and `practice()` runs only on explicit user click.
    export interface AcademyPanelOptions {
      /** Borrowed viewport container element into which the HUD reopen button and floating window are mounted. */
      readonly container: HTMLElement;
      /** Window factory used to create the pinned Academy floating window (`id: 'academy'`). */
      readonly windows: Pick<WindowSystem, 'create'>;
      /** Window shell used to register the Academy dock instrument button. */
      readonly shell: Pick<WindowShell, 'addInstrument'>;
      /** Optional local storage backend for lesson completion progress. */
      readonly storage?: ThemePreferences;
      /** Learner/seat identifier appended to the course storage key (`<course.id>/v<course.version>/<learnerKey>`). */
      readonly learnerKey: string;
      /** Returns a detached `AcademyFlight` snapshot for the currently selected owned ship, or `undefined` when no owned ship is selected. */
      read(): AcademyFlight | undefined;
      /** Actual current bindings, resolved by the host; never hard-coded by lessons. */
      bindings(): string;
      /** Explicit button action only. The host checks local authority/ownership again. */
      practice(setup: AcademyPractice): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

## AcademyPanel
Mounted Orbital Academy UI panel and world-space target orbit overlay source. Disposing removes the HUD button, disposes the dock instrument, window and tutorial runner.
    export interface AcademyPanel extends Disposable {
      /**
       * Advances UI and tutorial runner state by `elapsed` wall-clock seconds (throttled internally to 0.1 s intervals).
       * Reads `options.read()` and never mutates simulation state.
       */
      update(elapsed: number): void;
      /**
       * Returns world-space `OverlayPrimitive` line segments (in metres) for the suggested target orbit when a lesson with a target
       * is active, un-interrupted, checked by the learner and orbiting the viewed body; otherwise returns `[]`. Reuses preallocated line objects.
       */
      draw(view: PresentationView): readonly OverlayPrimitive[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.academy-ui.

## AcademyPanelFactory
Factory creating mounted `AcademyPanel` instances (`learning.academy@1.0.0`). Candidate-owned: disposing the provider disposes all active panels and rejects subsequent `create()` calls.
    export interface AcademyPanelFactory {
      /** Mounts the Academy window, dock instrument, HUD objective button and tutorial runner. Throws if the provider is disposed. */
      create(options: AcademyPanelOptions): AcademyPanel;
    }

=== game-api: universe.ts [api-reference/game-api/universe]
Declarations from universe.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/universe.ts.

## SOI
Sphere-of-influence radii in metres (`radius`, optional hysteresis `entryMetres` and `exitMetres`) for gravitational handoffs.
    export interface SOI {
      readonly radius: number;
      readonly entryMetres?: number;
      readonly exitMetres?: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## Ephemeris
Prescribed celestial body trajectory relative to its parent body: fixed inertial coordinates (`m`, `m/s`) or circular Keplerian orbit (`m`, `rad`, `s`).
    export type Ephemeris =
      | {
          readonly kind: 'fixed-inertial';
          readonly position?: readonly [number, number];
          readonly velocity?: readonly [number, number];
        }
      | {
          readonly kind: 'circular-kepler';
          readonly radius?: number;
          readonly radiusMetres?: number;
          readonly phaseRadiansAtEpoch?: number;
          readonly direction?: number;
          readonly period?: number;
        };
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## CelestialBodyDefinition
Authored celestial body definition with SI physical parameters (`radius`/`radiusMetres` in `m`, `mass` in `kg`, `mu` in `m³/s²`), ephemeris, terrain, and appearance.
    export interface CelestialBodyDefinition {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly parentId?: string | null;
      readonly radius?: number;
      readonly radiusMetres?: number;
      readonly mass?: number; // kg
      readonly mu?: number; // m^3 / s^2
      readonly soi?: SOI;
      readonly color?: string;
      readonly accentColor?: string;
      readonly atmosphereColor?: string;
      readonly horizonColor?: string;
      readonly skyColor?: string;
      readonly surfaceFeatures?: 'globe-lines' | 'craters' | 'smooth' | 'star';
      readonly surface?: SurfaceDefinition;
      readonly ephemeris?: Ephemeris;
      /** Sidereal spin; absent bodies do not rotate. */
      readonly rotation?: RotationDefinition;
      /** How the body is drawn; derived from `surfaceFeatures` and its colours when absent. */
      readonly appearance?: BodyAppearance;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## AppearanceStyle
Procedural look of a body. Presentation only: it never changes the physical outline.
    export type AppearanceStyle = 'rocky' | 'arid' | 'oceanic' | 'banded' | 'icy' | 'hazy' | 'volcanic' | 'star';
Used by: spacelab.planet-designer, spacelab.render-webgl.

## BodyAppearance
Presentation-only procedural shader appearance parameters for a celestial body (style, `#rrggbb` palette, seed in `[0, 1000]`, and `[0, 1]` tuning sliders).
    export interface BodyAppearance {
      readonly style: AppearanceStyle;
      /** Up to four '#rrggbb' colours. Their roles depend on the style:
       * rocky [ground, dark lava seas, bright highlands, polar frost], arid [sand, rock, salt flats, polar ice],
       * oceanic [ocean, vegetation, desert, ice and cloud],
       * banded [band, dark band, light band, storms], icy [ice, cracks, frost, tint], hazy [cloud, dark cloud, light cloud, tint],
       * volcanic [rock, dark crust, lava, sulfur], star [core, surface, limb, spots]. */
      readonly colors?: readonly string[];
      /** Pattern seed, 0 to 1000; derived from the body id when absent. */
      readonly seed?: number;
      /** Tuning from 0 to 1, 0.5 by default: sea level (or dry basins), cloud cover, polar ice,
       * crater density, turbulence of bands and clouds, and volcanic glow. */
      readonly water?: number;
      readonly clouds?: number;
      readonly ice?: number;
      readonly craters?: number;
      readonly turbulence?: number;
      readonly glow?: number;
    }
Used by: spacelab.planet-designer.

## AppearanceTuning
Tunable appearance settings.
    export type AppearanceTuning = 'water' | 'clouds' | 'ice' | 'craters' | 'turbulence' | 'glow';
Used by: spacelab.planet-designer.

## APPEARANCE_TUNING

## RotationDefinition
Authored sidereal rotation specification for a celestial body (`period` in `s`, `direction` `-1` CW or `1` CCW, `phaseRadiansAtEpoch` in `rad`).
    export interface RotationDefinition {
      /** Seconds per turn. */
      readonly period: number;
      /** -1 (default) turns clockwise like default orbits, so a prograde launch gains the spin. */
      readonly direction?: number;
      readonly phaseRadiansAtEpoch?: number;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## ResolvedBody
Fully validated celestial body with resolved SI physical constants (`radius` in `m`, `mass` in `kg`, `mu` in `m³/s²`), SOI hysteresis radii, surface identity, and 4-color appearance.
    export interface ResolvedBody extends Body {

      readonly id: string;

      readonly horizonColor?: string;
      readonly skyColor?: string;
      readonly surfaceFeatures: 'globe-lines' | 'craters' | 'smooth' | 'star';

    }

## LoadedSystem

=== game-api: universe-saving.ts [api-reference/game-api/universe-saving]
Declarations from universe-saving.ts used by documented modules.

Import these names from @space-sim/game-api. Units are SI unless a note says otherwise. Source: packages/game-api/src/universe-saving.ts.

## UniverseSaveFormat
Host universe container package codec interface (`core.universe-save-format@1.0.0`). Encodes WorldSave structures into zip-compressed .spaceuniverse archives and decodes byte buffers. Decoding is inert and never executes saved module scripts or activates unapproved packages. Throws when archive format is unrecognised, corrupted, or when abort is signalled.
    export interface UniverseSaveFormat {
      encode(save: WorldSave, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>;
      decode(bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<unknown>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

## CORE_UNIVERSE_FORMAT
Service key for host universe container package codec (`core.universe-save-format@1.0.0`).
    export const CORE_UNIVERSE_FORMAT: ServiceKey<UniverseSaveFormat> = Object.freeze({slot:'core.universe-save-format',version:'1.0.0'});
Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

## UniverseSaveSource
Snapshot of session and world identity captured prior to asynchronous save or load IO. Holds read-only world access, optional snapshot configuration, and permission flags.
    export interface UniverseSaveSource {
      /** Stable session/world identity; captured before asynchronous work. */
      readonly identity: object;
      readonly world: WorldRead;
      readonly options?: SavedWorldOptions;
      readonly canSave: boolean;
      readonly canLoad: boolean;
    }
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## UniverseSaveOptions
Host callbacks and session access supplied when instantiating a UniverseSaveCommands controller. Defines current world resolution and atomic, guarded state restoration.
    export interface UniverseSaveOptions {
      current(): UniverseSaveSource;
      /** Prepare exact installed packages, validate state, then atomically publish. Call
       * guard immediately before publication; observe signal during asynchronous work. */
      restore(raw: unknown, signal: AbortSignal, guard: () => void): Promise<void>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

## UniverseSaveCommands
Single-operation universe save and load command controller (`universe.saves@1.0.0`). Serializes save/load actions, validates session consistency across async boundaries, and delegates payload encoding/decoding without activating untrusted scripts. Throws if another save/load is already in flight, if permissions are denied, or on session drift.
    export interface UniverseSaveCommands extends Disposable {
      save(write: (name: string, bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<void>, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<WorldSave>;
      load(read: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

## UniverseSaveFactory
Factory creating cancellable single-operation universe save and load command controllers (`universe.saves@1.0.0`).
    export interface UniverseSaveFactory { create(options: UniverseSaveOptions): UniverseSaveCommands }
Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

## UNIVERSE_SAVES
Service key for the universe save/load command controller factory (`universe.saves@1.0.0`).
    export const UNIVERSE_SAVES: ServiceKey<UniverseSaveFactory> = Object.freeze({slot:'universe.saves',version:'1.0.0'});
Used by: spacelab.universe-saves.

## UniverseFiles
Bounded browser file dialog and download management service (`universe.files@1.0.0`). Reads user-selected Blobs up to 64 MiB and writes download files via temporary object URLs. Created URLs are automatically revoked after a 10-second timeout or immediately upon provider disposal. Throws if file sizes exceed 64 MiB, filenames contain invalid characters, or if aborted.
    export interface UniverseFiles extends Disposable {
      read(file: Blob, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>;
      write(name: string, bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.universe-files, spacelab.universe-save-controls.

## UNIVERSE_FILES
Service key for the browser universe file reader and writer (`universe.files@1.0.0`).
    export const UNIVERSE_FILES: ServiceKey<UniverseFiles> = Object.freeze({slot:'universe.files',version:'1.0.0'});
Used by: spacelab.universe-files, spacelab.universe-save-controls.

## UniverseSaveControls
Interactive Save universe and Load universe DOM control instance (`ui.universe-saves@1.0.0`). Reports busy status during active operations and restores original DOM on disposal.
    export interface UniverseSaveControls extends Disposable { readonly busy: boolean }
Used by: spacelab.universe-save-controls.

## UniverseSaveControlOptions
Configuration options for mounting Save universe and Load universe controls into a host DOM element. Allows borrowing caller-supplied buttons or generating standard controls with an optional prefix.
    export interface UniverseSaveControlOptions {
      readonly root: HTMLElement;
      readonly commands: UniverseSaveCommands;

      readonly prefix?: string;

      loaded?(): void;
      error?(operation: 'save' | 'load', error: unknown): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.universe-save-controls.

## UniverseDisk
Low-level server filesystem IO operations required by server universe storage (`core.universe-disk@1.0.0`). Native operations only. Editable server storage owns naming, serialization, queueing, recovery and retention; the host owns filesystem access and engine validation.
    export interface UniverseDisk {
      join(...parts: string[]): string;
      read(path: string): Promise<string>;
      directory(path: string): Promise<void>;
      names(path: string): Promise<readonly string[]>;
      rename(from: string, to: string): Promise<void>;
      copy(from: string, to: string): Promise<void>;
      remove(path: string): Promise<void>;
      replace(path: string, text: string): Promise<void>;
      replaceSync(path: string, text: string): void;
      missing(error: unknown): boolean;
    }

## ServerUniverseOptions
Configuration options for initializing a ServerUniverseStore instance. Defines target directory, backup retention count, state restore logic, and configuration error classification.
    export interface ServerUniverseOptions<T> {
      readonly directory: string; readonly keepBackups: number;
      restore(raw: unknown): T | Promise<T>;
      /** Missing/incompatible packages require user recovery, not quarantine or fallback. */
      configurationError(error: unknown): boolean;
      log?(message: string): void;
    }
Used by: spacelab.universe-server-storage.

## ServerUniverseStore
Persistent server universe storage managing world.json persistence, atomic writes, and backups. Quarantines unparseable files, falls back to timestamped backups, and preserves existing worlds on configuration error. Enqueues asynchronous writes in arrival order; idle() drains the queue prior to shutdown.
    export interface ServerUniverseStore<T> extends Disposable {
      readonly file: string; readonly backups: string;
      load(): Promise<{readonly loaded: T; readonly from: string} | undefined>;
      save(save: WorldSave): Promise<void>;
      saveSync(save: WorldSave): void;
      idle(): Promise<void>;
      backup(): Promise<string | undefined>;
      backupFiles(): Promise<string[]>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.universe-server-storage.

## ServerUniverseStorage
Factory creating persistent server universe stores for headless Node environments (`universe.server-storage@1.0.0`).
    export interface ServerUniverseStorage { create<T>(options: ServerUniverseOptions<T>): ServerUniverseStore<T> }

=== game-api: vehicles.ts [api-reference/game-api/vehicles]

## TRAINER_VEHICLE_ID
Legacy craft map to this definition without changing state or old save bytes.
    export const TRAINER_VEHICLE_ID = 'spacelab.trainer/orbit-trainer';
Used by: spacelab.trainer, spacelab.trainer-appearance.

## VehicleConfigurationError
A valid universe needs installed vehicle packages, rather than damaged-file recovery.
    export class VehicleConfigurationError extends Error {}
Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## VehicleDefinition
Immutable authoritative handling, in SI units. Omitted handling uses the selected system's vehicleDefaults, preserving the trainer's existing configuration precedence.
    export interface VehicleDefinition {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly handling?: Readonly<VehicleDefaults>;
    }
Used by: spacelab.trainer.

## VehicleCatalog
Explicit opt-in for mixed-vehicle saves/sessions. Definitions come from enabled, approved modules; saved definitions never install or execute missing packages.
    export interface VehicleCatalog {
      readonly format: 'space-sim-vehicles/1';
      readonly defaultId: string;
      readonly definitions: readonly VehicleDefinition[];
    }
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## VehicleAppearance
Screen-space vector artwork (+X forward), shared by Canvas and WebGL. The hull is a triangle fan and a closed outline. The flame tip extends with normalized throttle. This optional presentation data never changes authority or physical geometry.
    export interface VehicleAppearance {
      /** Namespaced vehicle identifier matching a declared VehicleDefinition id. */
      readonly vehicleId: string;
      /** Hull vertices in screen coordinates relative to the craft center (3 to 32 points). */
      readonly hull: readonly Vec[];
      /** Optional thruster exhaust flame dimensions for active acceleration visuals. */
      readonly flame?: { readonly x: number; readonly halfWidth: number; readonly length: number; readonly throttleLength: number };
    }
Used by: spacelab.render-canvas, spacelab.render-webgl, spacelab.trainer-appearance.

## VehicleArtwork
Lookup table resolving optional vector appearance artwork by namespaced vehicle ID.
    export interface VehicleArtwork {
      /** Retrieve artwork for a vehicle ID, or undefined to fall back to generic VEHICLE_MARKER. */
      get(vehicleId?: string): VehicleAppearance | undefined;
    }
Used by: spacelab.trainer-appearance.

## vehicleId
Validate and return a namespaced vehicle identifier (`<module-id>/<local-name>`, max 160 chars). Throws if malformed.
    export function vehicleId(value: unknown): string;
Used by: spacelab.session-flight.

## captureVehicleDefinition
Validate, bound SI handling parameters (`acceleration` in `m/s²`, `turnRate` in `rad/s`, `initialThrottle` in `[0, 1]`), and freeze a `VehicleDefinition`.

Used by: host or creator modules (not imported by shipped modules).

## captureVehicleCatalog
Validate, sort by ID, and freeze a `space-sim-vehicles/1` catalog (1–128 unique vehicle definitions). Throws on duplicate or missing `defaultId`.

Used by: spacelab.session-flight.
